\section{Case I: $\eta$ = 1}
    In Case I, charm, anti-charm, and light quarks are localized into the inner region, charm and light quarks into the middle region and only light quarks in the outermost region. 
        \subsection{ Calculation of Kinetic Energy }
            \begin{equation}
            K.E = \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} d^3r \frac{3(6\pi^2\hbar^3)^\frac{5}{3}}{20\pi^2\hbar^3m^I(g^I)^\frac{2}{3}} \bigl(n^I(r)\bigr)^\frac{5}{3} + \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} d^3r \frac{3(6\pi^2\hbar^3)^\frac{5}{3}}{20\pi^2\hbar^3\overline{m}^I (\overline{g^I})^\frac{2}{3}} \bigl(\overline{n^I(r)}\bigr)^\frac{5}{3}
            \label{23}
            \end{equation}
            We have, 
            \begin{equation}
            n^I(r) = \biggl[\frac{2 \times (4/3)\alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^\frac{3}{2} \biggl(\frac{f^I(r)}{r}\biggr)^\frac{3}{2} \frac{g^I}{6\pi^2} 
            \label{24}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation}
            \overline{n^I(r)} = \biggl[\frac{2 \times (4/3)\alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^\frac{3}{2} \biggl(\frac{\overline{f^I(r)}}{r}\biggr)^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{g}^I}{6\pi^2}
            \label{25}
            \end{equation}
            So that, 
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            K.E  &= \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} d^3r \frac{3(6\pi^2\hbar^3)^\frac{5}{3}}{20\pi^2\hbar^3m^I(g^I)^\frac{2}{3}} \biggl[\biggl[\frac{2 \times (4/3)\alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^\frac{3}{2} \biggl(\frac{f^I(r)}{r}\biggr)^\frac{3}{2} \frac{g^I}{6\pi^2} \biggr]^\frac{5}{3} \\ & \quad + \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} d^3r \frac{3(6\pi^2\hbar^3)^\frac{5}{3}}{20\pi^2\hbar^3\overline{m}^I (\overline{g^I})^\frac{2}{3}} \biggl[\biggl[\frac{2 \times (4/3)\alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^\frac{3}{2} \biggl(\frac{\overline{f^I(r)}}{r}\biggr)^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{g}^I}{6\pi^2}\biggr]^\frac{5}{3}
            \label{26}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation}
            K.E = K.E_1 + K.E_2 
            \end{equation}
            Taking $K.E_1$, 
            \begin{equation*}
            K.E_1 = \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} d^3r \frac{3(6\pi^2\hbar^3)^\frac{5}{3}}{20\pi^2\hbar^3m^I(g^I)^\frac{2}{3}} \biggl[\biggl[\frac{2 \times (4/3)\alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^\frac{3}{2} \biggl(\frac{f^I(r)}{r}\biggr)^\frac{3}{2} \frac{g^I}{6\pi^2} \biggr]^\frac{5}{3}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} d^3r \frac{3(6\pi^2\hbar^3)^\frac{5}{3}}{20\pi^2\hbar^3m^I(g^I)^\frac{2}{3}} \biggl(\frac{g^I}{6\pi^2}\biggr)^\frac{5}{3} \biggl[\frac{2 \times (4/3) \alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^{\frac{3}{2} \times \frac{5}{3}} \biggl(\frac{f^I(r)}{r}\biggr)^{\frac{3}{2} \times \frac{5}{3}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \sum_I\int^{r_{max}}d^3r \frac{3\hbar^{3\times \frac{5}{3}}(g^I)^{\frac{5}{3}-\frac{2}{3}}}{20\pi^2 \hbar^3m^I}\biggl[\frac{2\times(4/3)\alpha_s}{a} \biggr]^\frac{5}{2}\biggl(\frac{f^I(r)}{r}\biggr)^\frac{5}{2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \frac{3\hbar^2}{20 \pi^2 m_1} \biggl[\frac{2 \times(\frac{4}{3}) \alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^\frac{5}{2} 4\pi \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} dr r^2 \alpha_I g^I \biggl(\frac{f_I(r)}{r}\biggr)^\frac{5}{2} 
            \end{equation*}
            where, 
            $\alpha_I = \frac{m_1}{m_I}$
            \begin{equation*}
            = constant.\sum_I \int^{r_{max}} dr r^2 \alpha_I g^I \biggl(\frac{f_I(r)}{r}\biggr)^\frac{5}{2} 
            \end{equation*}
            Put $r=Rx$, $dr = Rdx$
            \begin{equation*}
            = constant. \sum_I \int^{x_{max}} Rdx R^2 x^2 \alpha_I g^I \biggl(\frac{f^I(x)}{Rx}\biggr)^\frac{5}{2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = constant. \sum_I \int^{x_{max}} dx R^{3 - \frac{5}{2}} \alpha_I g^I \frac{(f^I(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{x^{\frac{5}{2}-2}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = constant\sqrt{R}. \sum_I \int^{x_{max}} dx \alpha_I g^I \frac{(f^I(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            K.E_1 = C \sum_I \int^{x_{max}} dx \alpha_I g^I \frac{(f^I(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \frac{K.E_1}{C} = \sum_I \int^{x_{max}} dx \alpha_I g^I \frac{(f^I(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \alpha_1 g_1 \int^{x_1}_0 dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_2 g_2 \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \alpha_1 g_1 \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_1 g_1 \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_1 g_1 \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_2 g_2 \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_2 g_2 \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \alpha_1 g_1 (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_1 g_1 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2}\int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_1 g_1 \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_2 g_2 (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2}\int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \alpha_2 g_2 \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation}
            \frac{K.E_1}{C} = \bigl[\alpha_1 g_1 ( \beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2 g_2 (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2}\bigr] \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \bigl[ \alpha_I g_1 ( \gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2 g_2 \bigr] \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_1 g_1 \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
            \label{27}
            \end{equation}
            Taking $K.E_2$, 
            \begin{equation}
            K.E_2 =  \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} d^3r \frac{3(6\pi^2\hbar^3)^\frac{5}{3}}{20\pi^2\hbar^3\overline{m}^I (\overline{g^I})^\frac{2}{3}} \biggl[\biggl[\frac{2 \times (4/3)\alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^\frac{3}{2} \biggl(\frac{\overline{f^I(r)}}{r}\biggr)^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{g}^I}{6\pi^2}\biggr]^\frac{5}{3}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation*}
            =  \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} d^3r \frac{3(6\pi^2\hbar^3)^\frac{5}{3}}{20\pi^2\hbar^3\overline{m}^I (\overline{g^I})^\frac{2}{3}} \biggl(\frac{\overline{g^I}}{6\pi^2}\biggr)^\frac{5}{3} \biggl[\frac{2 \times (4/3)\alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^{\frac{3}{2} \times \frac{5}{3}} \biggl(\frac{\overline{f^I(r)}}{r}\biggr)^{\frac{3}{2} \times \frac{5}{3}} 
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
             = \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} d^3r \frac{3\hbar^2 \overline{g^I}}{20 \pi^2 \overline{m^I}} \biggl[\frac{2 \times (4/3)\alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^\frac{5}{2} \biggl(\frac{\overline{f^I(r)}}{r}\biggr)^\frac{5}{2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
             = \frac{3\hbar^2}{20 \pi^2} \biggl[\frac{2 \times (4/3) \alpha_s}{a} \biggr]^\frac{5}{2} \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} d^3r \frac{\overline{g^I}}{\overline{m^I}} \biggl( \frac{\overline{f^I(r)}}{r}\biggr)^\frac{5}{2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
             = \frac{3\hbar^2}{20\pi^2 m_1} \biggl[\frac{2 \times (4/3) \alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^\frac{5}{2} 4 \pi \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} dr r^2 \overline{\alpha_I} \overline{g^I} \biggl(\frac{\overline{f^I(r)}}{r}\biggr)^\frac{5}{2} 
            \end{equation*}
            where, 
            $\overline{\alpha_I} = \frac{m_1}{\overline{m_I}}$
            \begin{equation*}
            = constant. \sum_I \int^{r_{max}} dr r^2 \overline{\alpha_I} \overline{g^I} \biggl(\frac{\overline{f^I(r)}}{r}\biggr)^\frac{5}{2}
            \end{equation*}
            Put $r=Rx$, $dr= Rdx$ 
            \begin{equation*}
            = constant. \sum_I \int^{x_{max}} Rdx R^2 x^2 \overline{\alpha_I} \overline{g_I} \biggl(\frac{\overline{f^I(x)}}{Rx}\biggr)^\frac{5}{2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = constant. \sum_I \int^{x_{max}} dx \sqrt{R} \overline{\alpha_I} \overline{g_I}\frac{(\overline{f^I(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = constant \sqrt{R}. \sum_I \int^{x_{max}} dx \overline{\alpha_I} \overline{g_I} \frac{(\overline{f^I(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            K.E_2 = C \sum_I \int^{x_{max}} dx \overline{\alpha_I} \overline{g_I} \frac{(\overline{f^I(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \frac{K.E_2}{C} = \sum_I \int^{x_{max}} dx \overline{\alpha_I} \overline{g_I} \frac{(\overline{f^I(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation}
            \frac{K.E_2}{C} = \overline{\alpha_2} \overline{g_2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \label{28}
            \end{equation}
            Adding eqns \ref{27} and \ref{28}, 
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            \frac{K.E_1}{C} + \frac{K.E_2}{C} &= \bigl[ \alpha_1g_1 (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2 g_2 (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2}\bigr] \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \bigl[ \alpha_1 g_1 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2g_2 \bigr] \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \alpha_1 g_1 \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \overline{\alpha_2} \overline{g_2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \bigl[ \alpha_1g_1 (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2 g_2 (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \overline{\alpha_2}\overline{g_2} \bigr] \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \bigl[ \alpha_1 g_1 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2g_2 \bigr] \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_1 g_1 \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}  
            \end{equation*}
            From appendix I, 
            \begin{equation}
            \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} =   \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5\overline{N_2}}{21\eta} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}
            \label{29}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}}dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} &=   \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5}{7Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \Bigg[\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta}  +\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} - \\ & \quad (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3\eta}\Biggr] +   \frac{5}{7Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}
            \label{30}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} &=   \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{4}{7} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2}\sqrt{x}(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_2}  -   \frac{5}{7Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_1}\Biggl[ \frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta} + \gamma_{12} \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3\eta} - (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_2 Q_1}{3\eta} \\ & \quad + \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta}\bigl[(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}  - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\bigr]   - (\gamma_{12}) (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta} + (\gamma_{12})\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}\Biggr]  \\ & \quad +   \frac{5}{7Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^2 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggl[ \frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} + \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}\Biggr] 
            \label{31}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation}
            So that,
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            \frac{K.E_1}{C} + \frac{K.E_2}{C} &= \bigl[ \alpha_1g_1 (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2 g_2 (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \overline{\alpha_2}\overline{g_2} \bigr] \Biggl[\frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x}  (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}  -  \frac{5\overline{N_2}}{21\eta} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}\Biggr]  + \bigl[ \alpha_1 g_1 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2g_2 \bigr]\\ & \quad\Biggl[  \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2}  -   \frac{4}{7}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5}{7Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \Bigg[\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta}  + \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} - \\ & \quad (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3\eta}\Biggr] +   \frac{5}{7Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2  
            \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}\Biggr]+ \alpha_1 g_1 \Biggl[  \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_1}  -   \frac{4}{7} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2}\sqrt{x}(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad -   \frac{5}{7Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_1}\Biggl[ \frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta}  + \gamma_{12} \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3\eta}  - (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_2 Q_1}{3\eta}  + \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta}\bigl[(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}  - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\bigr]  \\ & \quad - (\gamma_{12}) (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta} + (\gamma_{12})\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}\Biggr]   +   \frac{5}{7Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^2 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggl[ \frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} + \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}  \\ & \quad - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}\Biggr] \Biggr]
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &=   \frac{4}{7}\biggl[\alpha_1g_1(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2 g_2 (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \overline{\alpha_2}\overline{g_2}\biggr] \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} - \frac{5\overline{N_2}}{21\eta}\biggl[ \alpha_1g_1 (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2 g_2 (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} +  \overline{\alpha_2}\overline{g_2}\biggr]\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad + \frac{4}{7} \biggl[\alpha_1 g_1(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2}  +   \alpha_2 g_2 \biggr] \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{4}{7}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \bigl[\alpha_1g_1(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} +   \alpha_2g_2 \bigr] \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}   - \frac{5}{7Q_2} \biggl[\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} \\ & \quad +\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}   - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta} \biggr] \bigl[\alpha_1 g_1 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2}  +   \alpha_2 g_2 \bigr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} + \frac{5}{7Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}\bigl[\alpha_1g_1(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2}  +   \alpha_2 g_2 \bigr] \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{4}{7} \alpha_1 g_1 \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_1}  -  \frac{4}{7} \alpha_1g_1 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2}\sqrt{x}(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_2}  - \frac{5\alpha_1 g_1}{7 Q_1} \biggl[\frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta} 
            + \gamma_{12} \frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} - (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_2Q_1}{3\eta} \\ & \quad + (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta}  - (\gamma_{12})(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta} +  (\gamma_{12})\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}\biggr]\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_1} + \frac{5}{7Q_1}(\gamma_{12})^2 \alpha_{1}g_{1}\biggl[\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} \biggr. \\ & \quad \biggl. + \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} -   (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}\biggr]\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            \frac{(K.E_1+K.E_2)}{C} & = \frac{4}{7} \bigl[\alpha_1g_1(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2 g_2(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \overline{\alpha_2}\overline{g_2} - \alpha_1g_1(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} - \alpha_2g_2\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2}\bigr]\sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}  \\ & \quad + \frac{4}{7} \bigl[\alpha_1g_1(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} +   \alpha_2g_2  -\alpha_1g_1(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2}\bigr]\sqrt{x}(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{4}{7} \alpha_1g_1\sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_1} + \frac{5\overline{N_2}}{21\eta} \biggl[\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \\ & \quad \frac{\overline{Q_2}}{Q_2}\bigl[\alpha_1 g_1(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2 g_2 \bigr]  
            - \alpha_1 g_1 (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} -\alpha_2 g_2 (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2}-   \overline{\alpha_2}\overline{g_2}\biggr]\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} 
            + \frac{5}{21\eta}\biggl[\frac{(\gamma_{12})^2 \alpha_{1}g_{1}}{Q_1}  \\ & \quad \biggl[N_2Q_2 + \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\overline{N_2}Q_2\biggr] - \bigl[\alpha_1g_1 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2g_2 \bigr]  \biggl[N_2 +  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{Q_2}-(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \overline{N_2}\biggr]\biggr]\\ & \quad \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{5\alpha_1g_1}{21\eta}\biggl[N_1 + \gamma_{12} \frac{N_2Q_2}{Q_1}  + (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\bigl[(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\overline{N_2} - N_2\bigr] - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\overline{N_2}  - (\gamma_{12})(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{Q_1} \biggr. \\ & \quad +   \biggl.(\gamma_{12})\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{Q_1}\biggr]\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_1}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            Therefore, the final expression of kinetic energy is
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            K.E &= C\Biggl[\frac{4}{7} \biggl[\alpha_1g_1\bigl[(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} - (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \bigr] +  \overline{\alpha_2}\overline{g_2}\biggr] \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{4}{7} \alpha_2g_2\sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} +  \frac{4}{7} \alpha_1g_1 \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad + \frac{5\overline{N_2}}{21\eta} \biggl[\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2\frac{\overline{Q_2}}{Q_2}\bigl[\alpha_1g_1(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_2g_2\bigr] - \alpha_1g_1 (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} -\alpha_2g_2 (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} -  \overline{\alpha_2}\overline{g_2}\biggr]\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} + \frac{5}{21\eta} \biggl[\frac{(\gamma_{12})^2 \alpha_{1}g_{1}}{Q_1} \\ & \quad \biggl[N_2Q_2  +\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}- (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \overline{N_2}Q_2\biggr]-\biggl[\alpha_1g_1(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} +  \alpha_2g_2\biggr]\biggl[N_2+\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{Q_2} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\overline{N_2}\biggr]\biggr]\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad - \frac{5\alpha_1g_1}{21\eta}\biggl[ N_1 + \gamma_{12}\frac{N_2Q_2}{Q_1} + (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \biggl[(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\overline{N_2} - N_2\biggr]- (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\overline{N_2} - (\gamma_{12})(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{Q_1} \\ & \quad +   (\gamma_{12})\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{Q_1}\biggr]\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_1}\Biggr]
            \end{split}
            \label{32}
            \end{equation}
            where, 
            \begin{equation}
            C = \frac{12\hbar^2\sqrt{R}}{20 \pi m_1}\biggl[\frac{2\times(4/3)\alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^\frac{5}{2}
            \end{equation}
        \subsection{Calculation of Potential Energy}
            The potential energy of Case I depends on seven different constants. The analytical calculation of each of these constants is derived below.  
        \subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{11}$}
            \begin{equation}
            K_{11} =\int^{x_1}_0 dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_1}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
            \label{92}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation}
            K_{11} = K_{11} \cdot 1 + K_{11} \cdot 2 
            \label{93}
            \end{equation}
            At first,
            \begin{equation}
            K_{11} \cdot 1 = \int^{x_1}_0 dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}
            \label{94}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}\int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)} \biggr] \biggr] + (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \\ & \quad + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2}  \biggl[x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr] \Biggr] + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \gamma_{12}  + \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr] \Biggr] 
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^3 \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \gamma_{12} + \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*} 
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \\ & \quad - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^3 \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \Biggr] \\ & \quad \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \gamma_{12} + \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12} \Biggr] \\ & \quad \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            The first, second, and third terms get canceled with $K_{11} \cdot 2$,
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            &= - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^3 \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \Biggr] \\ & \quad \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \gamma_{12} + \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12} \Biggr] \\ & \quad \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
            \end{split}
            \label{95}
            \end{equation}
            For convenience, above equation is evaluated in three sections separately.
            \begin{itemize}
            \item Section 1
            \end{itemize}
            \begin{equation}
            - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \label{96}
            \end{equation}
            Substituting values of above integration from appendix
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &=     - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} - \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] -   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} - \frac{5}{7Q_2} \Biggl[ \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta} \\ & \quad + \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) -  \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{5}{7Q_2} \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] -   \frac{1}{Q_1} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad -   \frac{4}{7} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2}  - \frac{5}{7Q_1} \Biggl[ \frac{N_1 Q_1}{3 \eta} + \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta} \gamma_{12} - \frac{N_2 Q_1}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_1}{3 \eta} \bigl[ (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \bigr] \\ & \quad - \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12} +   \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta} \gamma_{12} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \Biggr] \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  + \frac{5}{7 Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^2 \Biggl[ \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta} + \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) -   \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggr]
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &=   - \frac{4}{7} \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}}\sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +  \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta} \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7} \frac{(\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{4}{7 Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} (\gamma_{12})^3 \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5}{7 Q_2} \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta} \frac{(\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{5}{7 Q_2} \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) (\gamma_{12})^3 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{5}{7Q_2} \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^3 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad -  \frac{5}{7 Q_2} \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{12})^3 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{4}{7 Q_1} \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_1} +   \frac{4}{7 Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \frac{5}{7 Q_1} \frac{N_1 Q_1}{3 \eta} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad +  \frac{5}{7 Q_1} \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{1}{Q_1} \frac{5}{7 Q_1} \frac{N_2 Q_1}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \frac{5}{7 Q_1} \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_1}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad -   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -             \label{102}

            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            &=   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -    \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -    \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +  \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^3 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^3 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +   \frac{N_1}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} 
            \end{split}
            \label{104}
            \end{equation}
            Therefore, $K_{11} = K_{11} \cdot 1 + K_{11} \cdot 2$
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &=   \frac{4}{7 Q_2}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} (\gamma_{12})^3 \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3 \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +  \frac{4}{7 Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^3 \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad -   \frac{4}{7 Q_1} \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_1} +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{12})^3 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} + \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \\ & \quad - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)  + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^3  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{\overline{Q_2}}{{Q_2}^2} -    \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \Biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^3 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +    \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^3  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^3 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{5 N_2 Q_2 }{21 \eta {Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{12})^2 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  \\ & \quad -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{12})^2  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta} \frac{Q_2}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{12})^2 (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} + \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12}  \\ & \quad + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^3 - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^3 - \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^3  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \\ & \quad - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12}  - \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2}  + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} \frac{Q_2}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{12})^2 + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} \frac{\overline{Q_2}}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{12})^2  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \\ & \quad -   \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^2  \Biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{5 N_1}{21 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  +   \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta {Q_1}^2} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} Q_2}{21 \eta {Q_1}^2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_1}^2} \gamma_{12}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad + \Biggl[ \frac{N_1}{3 \eta Q_1} + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} \gamma_{12}  - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \gamma_{12} \\ & \quad +   \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12} \Biggr] \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*} 
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            K_{11} & = \frac{4}{7} \biggl[ \frac{ \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} (\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} -   \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \biggr] \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{4}{7} \biggl[\frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2}}{Q_1} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} \biggr] \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{4}{7 Q_1} \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad + \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}}  - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} + \frac{\overline{Q_2}}{{Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^3  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \Biggr] + \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \\ & \quad -   \frac{\overline{Q_2}}{{Q_2}^2}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{12})^3 \Biggr] \Biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}  + \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12}}{Q_1} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}(\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} + \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2}}{Q_1} \\ & \quad + \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} - \frac{\overline{Q_2}}{{Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^3  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12}}{Q_1}  + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_1} + \frac{\overline{Q_2}}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{12})^2  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) - \frac{Q_2}{{Q_1}^2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^2 \Biggr] \\ & \quad + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{(\gamma_{12})^3}{Q_2} - \frac{2(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2}}{Q_1} + \frac{Q_2}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{12})^2 \Biggr]  + \frac{5}{21 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^3 + \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{{Q_2}^2}(\gamma_{12})^3 \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^3 \\ & \quad - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{12})^2 - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{12})^2 \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)  +   \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{12})^2 (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] \Biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} + \Biggl[ \frac{N_1}{3 \eta Q_1} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} \gamma_{12} + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta Q_{1}} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \Biggl[ (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{Q_2}}{Q_1}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \gamma_{12}  + \frac{Q_2}{Q_1} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12} \Biggr] + \frac{5}{21 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{N_1}{Q_1} + \frac{N_2 Q_2}{{Q_1}^2} \gamma_{12} - \frac{N_2}{Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{Q_1} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{Q_1} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{{Q_1}^2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12}  + \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{{Q_1}^2} \gamma_{12}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \Biggr] \Biggr] \frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_1} 
            \end{split}
            \label{105}
            \end{equation} 
            \subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{22}$}
            \begin{equation}
            K_{22} = \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
            \label{48}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation}
            K_{22} = K_{22}\cdot 1 + K_{22} \cdot 2
            \label{49}
            \end{equation}
            At first, 
            \begin{equation}
            K_{22}\cdot 1 = \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2} 
            \label{50}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}\biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)}\biggr]\biggr] + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[\biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}  - \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2}\biggr] \biggl[x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)}\biggr]_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad+ \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x)\biggr]\Biggr]
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{x}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}}\int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} - \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \biggr] \biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)} \biggr]_{\overline{x_2}} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}}dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \frac{1}{Q_2}\int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - \frac{1}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            The two terms get canceled with $K_{22} \cdot 2$
            \begin{equation*}
            =\biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} - \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2}\biggr] \biggl[ x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)} \biggr]_{\overline{x_2}} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{1}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \frac{1}{Q_2}\biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} - \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \biggr] \biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)}\biggr]_{\overline{x_2}} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2} -  \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \biggl[\frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{5\overline{N_2}}{21\eta} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \biggr] \\ & \quad -   \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[\frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5}{7Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \biggl[ \frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} +\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta} \biggr] \\ & \quad +  \frac{5}{7Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} \biggr]
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &=\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta Q_2} \biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} -  \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7} \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}}\sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta} \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7 Q_2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +  \frac{4}{7Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{5}{7Q_2} \frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{5}{7Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{5}{7Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  -  \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{5}{7Q_2}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta Q_2} \biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} -   \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta Q_2} \biggl[ \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} -  \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2}{Q_2} \biggr]\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7} \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}}\sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad +  \frac{5\overline{N_2}}{21 \eta} \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7Q_2} \sqrt{x}(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{4}{7Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{5N_2}{21\eta Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +  \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} \\ & \quad \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &=   \frac{4}{7Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3 \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7 Q_2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} + \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \biggl[\frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2}{Q_2} -  \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}\biggr] \\ & \quad   \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}  +  \frac{5\overline{N_2}}{21 \eta} \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}}\frac{d \overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} + \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta Q_2} \biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}-    \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad  +   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +  \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2} }{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            &= \frac{4}{7} \biggl[\frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2}}{Q_{2}} - \frac{\left(\beta_{2\overline{2}}\right)^{3}}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\biggr] \sqrt{x}  (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{4}{7 Q_{2}} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}}+ \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} \biggl[ \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^{2} \frac{\overline{Q_{2}}}{Q_{2}^{2}} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_{2}} + \frac{5}{7 \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^{3} \\ & \quad - \frac{5 \overline{Q_{2}}}{7 Q_{2}^{2}} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^{2}   \biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \biggl[ \frac{5 N_{2}}{21 \eta Q_{2}} + \beta_{2\overline{2}} \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_{2}} \biggl[ (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{1}{2} - \frac{5}{7} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{1}{2} \biggr] - \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_{2}^{2}} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) + \frac{5 \overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{21 \eta Q_{2}^{2}} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)   \biggr] \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}}
            \label{51}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation}
            At second, 
            \begin{equation}
            K_{22} \cdot 2 = \int^{x_2}_0 dx\sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx'\frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
            \label{52}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_x dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_x dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &        =     (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} + \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] \\ & \quad +    \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \biggl[\frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\biggr]
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &=   \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} +   \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad -   \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x}(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{1}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            The two terms get canceled with $K_{22} \cdot 1$
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &=   \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +     \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} + \frac{1}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad -   \frac{ \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &=   (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_2} } \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \frac{d \overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{N_2}{3\eta} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  \\ & \quad -   (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} 
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            Therefore, 
            \begin{equation*}
            K_{22} = K_{22} \cdot 1 + K_{22} \cdot 2
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            K_{22} &= \frac{4}{7} \biggl[ \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2}}{Q_{2}} - \frac{\left(\beta_{2\overline{2}}\right)^{3}}{\overline{Q_{2}}}   \biggr] \sqrt{x}  (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{4}{7 Q_{2}} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}}+ \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} \biggl[ \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^{2} \frac{\overline{Q_{2}}}{Q_{2}^{2}} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_{2}} + \frac{5}{7 \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^{3} \\ & \quad - \frac{5 \overline{Q_{2}}}{7 Q_{2}^{2}} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^{2}   \biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \biggl[ \frac{5 N_{2}}{21 \eta Q_{2}} + \beta_{2\overline{2}} \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_{2}} \biggl[ (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{1}{2} - \frac{5}{7} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{1}{2} \biggr] - \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_{2}^{2}} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)  + \frac{5 \overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{21 \eta Q_{2}^{2}} \\ & \quad \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)   \biggr] \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^3\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_2} }   \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \frac{d \overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{N_2}{3\eta} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} 
            \end{split}
            \label{54}
            \end{equation}
            \subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{\overline{2}\overline{2}}$}
            \begin{equation}
            K_{\overline{2}\overline{2}} = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}dx \int^{x}_{0} \sqrt{x^\prime}(\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)})^\frac{3}{2}dx^{\prime} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}dx \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}} dx^\prime
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \biggl. \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} dx \biggl[ \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\biggl(x^\prime \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^{\prime}} - \overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)} \biggr)\biggr|_{x}\biggr] + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0}\sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} dx \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} \frac{d^{2}\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^{\prime^2}}dx^{\prime}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}dx \cdot \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\biggl( x^\prime\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^\prime} - \overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}   \biggr) \Bigg|_{x}+ \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} dx \biggl( \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^{\prime}}\biggr|_{x}\biggr)
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} dx \cdot \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\biggl( x^{\prime} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^{\prime}} - \overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}\biggr)\biggr|_{x} + \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^{\prime}}\biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \cdot\frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta}  - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^{\prime}} \biggr|_{x} dx
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0}\frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}dx + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta} \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}   \biggl[ \frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{5 \overline{N_{2}}}{21 \eta} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}}\biggr] + \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation}
            K_{\overline{2}\overline{2}} = -   \frac{4}{7\overline{Q_{2}}} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \frac{4 \overline{N_{2}}}{7 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}}
            \end{equation}
            \subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{12}$}
            \begin{equation}
            K_{12} =  \int^{x_1}_0 dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_1}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_x dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
            \label{69}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation}
            K_{12} = K_{12}\cdot 1  + K_{12} \cdot 2 
            \label{70}
            \end{equation}
            At first,
            \begin{equation}
            K_{12} \cdot 1 = \int^{x_1}_0 dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}  
            \label{71}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation*}
            K_{12} \cdot 1 = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            & = (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \biggl[ x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}-\overline{f_2(x)} \biggr] \Biggr] + ( \gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \biggl[ \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad  + \int^{x}_{\overline{x_2}} dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2} \biggr]   + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \biggl[ \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x}_{x_2} dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2} \biggr]
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &=\frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \Biggl[\int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}\cdot x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \cdot \overline{f_2(x)} \Biggr] + (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}\Biggl[\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad + \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl(x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x)\biggr) - \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2}\Biggr] + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} \Biggr]
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \cdot x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad+ \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \cdot x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            The first and fourth term get canceled with $K_{12} \cdot 2$ 
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad - \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}  + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            &=   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] -  \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] \\ & \quad -   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5}{7 Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggl[\frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta} + \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta} - \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] \\ & \quad +   \frac{5}{7Q_2} \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] -   \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \Biggl[ \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad -  \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}
            \label{72}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation} 
            At second,
            \begin{equation}
            K_{12} \cdot 2 = \int^{x_1}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_x dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
            \label{73}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \int^{\overline{x_2}}_x dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} \Biggr] + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_x dx' \frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x')}}{dx'^2} + \frac{1}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx' \frac{d^2f_2(x')}{dx'^2} \Biggr] +  \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{1}{Q_2}\frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Biggr]
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr]  + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad \Biggl[   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Biggr]
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            & =   (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] \\ & \quad - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}  +   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            The second term and fourth term get canceled with $K_{12}\cdot1$
            \begin{equation*}
            =   (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] +   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}   \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}\Biggr] + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \Biggl[\frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl(x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x)\biggr)_{x_2} \\ & \quad - \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \biggl(x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} -  \overline{f_2(x)} \biggr)_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \cdots + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \Biggl[\frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} + \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)- \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \biggr]-   \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \Biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \cdots + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{3\eta} + \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2 \overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} -  \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \Biggr]\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \cdots +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_2}(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2}(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation}
            =(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}   \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}\Biggr] +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_2}(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2}(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}
            \label{74}
            \end{equation}
            Therefore, $K_{12} = K_{12} \cdot 1 + K_{12} \cdot 2$
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &=  \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{1}{Q_2}\frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}\Biggr] - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_2}}(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[  \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] \\ & \quad -   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5}{7 Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggl[\frac{N_2Q_2}{3 \eta} + \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) - \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3 \eta}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] \\ & \quad +  \frac{5}{7Q_2} \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta}  \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] -
