  \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \Biggl[ \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}\Biggr] +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= -  \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7\overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} + \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta \overline{ Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad -   \frac{4}{7 Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{4}{7 Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +  \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}  \\ & \quad  -   \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  +   \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -  \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}  +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}  +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            K_{12} &=  \frac{4}{7Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2 } \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  +    \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad -   \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation}
            \subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{21}$}
            \begin{equation}
            K_{21} = \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_2}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
            \label{82}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation}
            K_{21} = K_{21} \cdot 1 + K_{21} \cdot 2 
            \label{83}
            \end{equation}
            At first, 
            \begin{equation}
            K_{21} \cdot 1 = \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}
            \label{84}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0} dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0} dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} 
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            = (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \biggl( x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)} \biggr) \Biggr] + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[ \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x}_{\overline{x_2}} dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr]
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \Biggl[\int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \cdot x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggr] + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}  \\ & \quad + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl( x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr)  - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \Biggr] 
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            The first term and fourth term get canceled with $K_{21} \cdot 2$
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)  \\ & \quad \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &= \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2} -  \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}  -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] -   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad -   \frac{4}{7} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{5}{7Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggl[\frac{N_2Q_2}{3 \eta} + \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) - \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] +  \frac{5}{7Q_2} \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \\ & \quad \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr]  - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2} 
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
            &=  \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5N_2}{21 \eta} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +  \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr] -   \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +  \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            &=  \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}  \\ & \quad (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{4}{7 Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{4}{7Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +  \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  -  \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}  \\ & \quad -   \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +  \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
            & =   \frac{4}{7Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{4}{7 Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}  -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -    \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad +  \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +  \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +  \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  -  \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}
            \end{split}
            \end{equation}
            At second,
            \begin{equation}
            K_{21} \cdot 2 = \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
            \label{86}
            \end{equation}
            \begin{equation*}
            = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
             \begin{split}
                                &= \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \int^{\overline{x_2}}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} \Biggr] + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad \Biggl[ \int^{x_2}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}  + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} \Biggr]
                                \end{split}
                            \end{equation*}
                            \begin{equation*}
                            \begin{split}
                                &= (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_{x} dx' \frac{(\overline{f_2(x')})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} \Biggr] \\ & \quad + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}  \Biggl[ (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} \Biggr]
                                \end{split}
                            \end{equation*}
                            \begin{equation*}
                            \begin{split}
                                &= (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[\frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_{x} dx' \frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x')}}{dx'^2} + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx' \frac{d^2f_2(x')}{dx'^2} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx' \frac{d^2f_1(x')}{dx'^2} \Biggr] \\ & \quad + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx' \frac{d^2f_2(x')}{dx'^2} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx' \frac{d^2f_1(x')}{dx'^2} \biggr] 
                                \end{split}
                            \end{equation*}
                            \begin{equation*}
                            \begin{split}
                                &= (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[   \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} +   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  -   \frac{\gamma_{12}}{Q_1} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggr] + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad -   \frac{\gamma_{12}}{Q_1} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggr]
                                \end{split}
                            \end{equation*}
                            \begin{equation*}
                            \begin{split}
                                & =  \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \\ & \quad +   \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_1} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x}(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12}}{Q_1} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{\gamma_{12}}{Q_1} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}
                                \end{split}
                            \end{equation*}
                            The second term and eighth term get canceled with $K_{21} \cdot 1$
                            \begin{equation*}
                            \begin{split}
                                &=  \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1}\\ & \quad (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}
                                \end{split}
                            \end{equation*}
                            \begin{equation}
                                = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} -   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \Biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} \gamma_{12} \biggl[ \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{1}{2}}{Q_2} -   \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}
                                \label{87}
                            \end{equation}
                            Therefore, $K_{21} = K_{21} \cdot 1 + K_{21} \cdot 2$
                           \begin{equation*}
                            \begin{split}
                                & =   \frac{4}{7Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{4}{7 Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}  -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -    \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad +  \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +  \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +  \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}  \\ & \quad \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}-  \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \\ & \quad -   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \Biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}  + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} \gamma_{12} \biggl[ \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{1}{2}}{Q_2} -   \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}
                                \end{split}
                            \end{equation*}
                        \begin{equation}
                        \begin{split}
                       K_{21} &=  \frac{4}{7Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{4}{7Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2 } \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2 \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  +    \frac{5 \overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{21 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad -   \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta {Q_2}^2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}
                       \end{split}
                       \end{equation}
            \subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{\overline{2}2}$}
            \begin{equation}
                    K_{\overline{2}2} = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx^{'} \sqrt{x^{'}} (f_2(x^{'}))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{x}dx^{'} \frac{(f_2(x^{'}))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^{'}}}
                    \label{42}
            \end{equation}
            Substituting values from the appendix 
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
             & =  \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}\biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}-\overline{f_2(x)}\biggr] + \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}\Biggl[\biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}-  \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2}\biggr]\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \\ & \quad +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}\Biggr]
             \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
               & = \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}\biggl[\int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}\overline{f_2(x)} \biggr]+ \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \biggl[\biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}  -   \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2}\biggr]\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}+ \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}\biggr]
               \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
                &= \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}\int ^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{x}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} +  \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}\biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}-\frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2}\biggr]\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \\ & \quad - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x}(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}+   \frac{1}{Q_2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}
                \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                =\int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}- \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2}\biggr]\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}+ \frac{1}{Q_2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x}(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}- \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta} \biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} -  \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2}\biggr]\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}\biggl[\frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x}(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}-  \frac{5\overline{N_2}}{21\eta}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}\biggr]
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                =  \frac{\overline{N_2}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{3\eta\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}(\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right))}{3\eta Q_2}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}+  \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}-  \frac{4(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^{\frac{3}{2}}}{7\overline{Q_2}}\sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}+   \frac{5\overline{N_2}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{21\eta\overline{Q_2}}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta} \biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}-\frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} +  \frac{5(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{7\overline{Q_2}}\biggr]\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}-  \frac{4(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{7\overline{Q_2}}\sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}+   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation}
                K_{\overline{2}2} = -   \frac{4}{7\overline{Q_2}}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{x}(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}+\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta} \biggl[\frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}}-\frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} +  \frac{5(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{7\overline{Q_2}}\biggr]\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}
                \label{43}
            \end{equation}
                                \subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{\overline{2}1}$}
                        \begin{equation}
                            K_{\overline{2}1} = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}
                            } \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
                            \label{60}
                        \end{equation}
            \begin{equation}
                K_{\overline{2}1} = K_{\overline{2}1}\cdot 1 + K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 2
                \label{61}
            \end{equation}
            At first,
            \begin{equation}
                K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 1 = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}
                            } \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}
                            \label{62}
                \end{equation}
                \begin{equation*}
                    = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \cdot \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)} \biggr]
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                    = \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \cdot x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                    = \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
                \end{equation*}
                The first term get canceled with $K_{{\overline{2}1}} \cdot 2$
                \begin{equation*}
                 = -   \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \Biggr]
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation}
                    = -   \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}
                    \label{63}
                \end{equation}
                At second, 
                \begin{equation}
                    K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 2 = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
                    \label{64}
                \end{equation}
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    &=   \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} - \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} +   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad -   \frac{\gamma_{12}}{Q_1} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggr]
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                The second term in the bracket get canceled with $K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 1$
                \begin{equation}
                    =   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_{2}} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}
                    \label{65}
                \end{equation}
                Therefore,
                \begin{equation*}
                    K_{\overline{2}1} = K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 1 + K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 2 
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    &= -   \frac{4}{7\overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +  \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}
                    +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +  \frac{\overline{N_2}(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_{2}} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}
                    \end{split}
                    \end{equation*}
                    \begin{equation*}
                    \begin{split}
                        &= -   \frac{4}{7\overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} + \Biggl[ \frac{5 \overline{N_2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} -   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_{2}} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \Biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} + \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_2}(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \\ & \quad -   \frac{\overline{N_2} \gamma_{12}}{3 \eta Q_1} \Biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}
                        \end{split}
                    \end{equation*}
                    \begin{equation}
                   \begin{split}
                       K_{\overline{2}1}  &= -   \frac{4}{7\overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} + \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{5}{7 \overline{Q_2}} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}+   \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_{2}} \Biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta}  \Biggl[\frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \\ & \quad -   \frac{\gamma_{12}}{Q_1} \Biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  +   \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}
                         \label{66}
                         \end{split}
            \end{equation}
\section{Case II: $1 < \eta \leq 10$}
In Case II, all quarks are localized into the inner region, anti-charm and light quarks into the middle region, and only light quarks in the outermost region. 
\subsection{ Calculation of Kinetic Energy }
\begin{equation}
  K.E= K.E_{1} + K.E_{2} 
\label{K1}
\end{equation}
where, 
\begin{equation*}
    K.E_{1} = \sum_{I}\int^{r_{max}} d^{3}r \frac{3(6\pi^{2}\hbar^{3})^\frac{5}{3}}{20\pi^{2}\hbar^{3}m^{I}g_{I}^\frac{2}{3}} (n^{I}(r))^\frac{5}{3}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    K.E_{2} = \sum_{I} \int^{r_{max}}d^{3}r \frac{3(6\pi^{2}\hbar^{3})^\frac{5}{3}}{20\pi^{2}\hbar^{3}\overline{m}^{I} (\overline{g_{I}})^\frac{2}{3}} (\overline{n^{I}(r)})^\frac{5}{3}
\end{equation*}
Since, 
\begin{equation}
    n^{I}(r) = \left( \frac{2\times\frac{4}{3}\alpha_{s}}{a}\right)^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{f_{I}(r)}{r}\right)^\frac{3}{2} \frac{g_{I}}{6\pi^{2}}
        \label{K2}
\end{equation}
\begin{equation*}
    \overline{n^{I}(r)} = \left( \frac{2\times\frac{4}{3}\alpha_{s}}{a}\right)^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\overline{f_{I}(r)}}{r}\right)^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{g_{I}}}{6\pi^{2}}
\end{equation*}
$K.E_{1}$ becomes
\begin{equation*}
    K.E_{1}= \frac{3(6\pi^{2}\hbar^{3})^\frac{5}{3}}{20 \pi^{2}\hbar^{3}} \sum_{I} \int^{r_{max}}d^{3}r \frac{(n^{I}(r))^\frac{5}{3}}{m^{I}g_{I}^\frac{2}{3}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{3(6\pi^{2}\hbar^{3})^\frac{5}{3}}{20 \pi^{2} \hbar^{3}} \left( \frac{2 \times \frac{4}{3} \alpha_{s}}{a}\right)^{ \frac{3}{2} \times \frac{5}{3} }\cdot\left( \frac{1}{6 \pi^{2}}\right)^\frac{5}{3} \sum_{I} \frac{\int^{r_{max}}d^{3}r \left( \frac{f_{I}(r)}{r}\right)^\frac{5}{2}g_{I}^\frac{5}{3}}{m^{I}g_{I}^\frac{2}{3}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{3(6\pi^{2}\hbar^{3})^\frac{5}{3}}{20 \pi^{2}\hbar^{3}m_{1}} \left( \frac{2 \times \frac{4}{3} \alpha_{s}}{a}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \left( \frac{1}{6\pi^{2}}\right)^\frac{5}{3} \sum_{I}\int^{r_{max}} \alpha_{I} g_{I} \left( \frac{f_{I}(r)}{r}\right)^\frac{5}{2} d^{3}r 
\end{equation*}
 where, $\alpha_{I} = \frac{m^{1}}{m^{I}}$
\begin{equation*}
    =  \frac{3(6\pi^{2}\hbar^{3})^\frac{5}{3}}{20 \pi^{2}\hbar^{3}m_{1}} \left( \frac{2 \times \frac{4}{3} \alpha_{s}}{a}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \left( \frac{1}{6\pi^{2}}\right)^\frac{5}{3} 4 \pi \sum_{I} \int^{r_{max}} dr r^{2}\alpha_{I}g_{I} \left( \frac{f_{I}(r)}{r}\right)^\frac{5}{2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
    K.E_{1} = \text{constant} \cdot \sum_{I} \int^{r_{max}} dr r^{2} \alpha_{I} g_{I} \left( \frac{f_{I}(r)}{r}\right)^\frac{5}{2}
\end{equation}
where, 
\[ \text{constant} = \frac{3 \left( 6 \pi^{2} \hbar^{3} \right)^\frac{5}{3}}{20 \pi^{2} \hbar^{3} m_{1}}  \left( \frac{2 \times \frac{4}{3}\alpha_{s}}{4} \right)^\frac{5}{2} \left( \frac{1}{6\pi^{2}}\right)^\frac{5}{3} \cdot 4 \pi  \] 
\\
Put   $r = Rx$
\[ dr = R dx \]
\begin{equation*}
    K.E_{1} = \text{constant} \cdot \sum_{I} \int^{x_{max}} R dx R^{2} x^{2} \alpha_{I} \left( \frac{f_{I}(x)}{Rx}\right)^\frac{5}{2} g_{I}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
     = \text{constant} \cdot \sum_{I} \int^{x_{max}} \sqrt{R} \alpha_{I} g_{I} \frac{\left( f_{I}(x)\right)^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} dx 
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \text{constant} \cdot \sqrt{R} \sum_{I} \int^{x_{max}} \alpha_{I} g_{I} \frac{\left( f_{I}(x)\right)^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} dx 
    \end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
     = C \cdot \sum_{I} \alpha_{I} g_{I} \int^{x_{max}} dx \frac{\left( f_{I}(x)\right)^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
     \end{equation}
where, $ C = \text{constant} \cdot \sqrt{R} $
\begin{equation*}
    \frac{K.E_{1}}{C} = \alpha_{2} g_{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{\left( f_{2}(x)\right)^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_{1} g_{1} \int^{x_{1}}_{0} dx \frac{\left( f_{1}(x)\right)^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    \frac{K.E_{1}}{C}  = \alpha_{2}g_{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{\left( f_{2}(x)\right)^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}  + \alpha_{1}g_{1} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{\left( f_{1}(x)\right)^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}  + \alpha_{1}g_{1} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{\left( f_{1}(x)\right)^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_{1}g_{1} \int^{x_{1}}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{\left( f_{1}(x)\right)^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    \frac{K.E_{1}}{C} = \alpha_{2}g_{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_{1}g_{1} (\beta_{12})^\frac{5}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{}\sqrt{x} + \alpha_{1} g_{1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \alpha_{1} g_{1} \int^{x_{1}}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_{1}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
Similarly, 
\begin{equation*}
    \frac{K.E_{2}}{C} = \overline{\alpha_{2}}\overline{g_{2}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
   =  \overline{\alpha_{2}} \overline{g_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \overline{\alpha_{2}}\overline{g_{2}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
     = \overline{\alpha_{2}} \overline{g_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{5}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \overline{\alpha_{2}} \overline{g_{2}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
Adding, 
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
    \frac{K.E_{1}}{C} + \frac{K.E_{2}}{C} & = \left[  \alpha_{1}g_{1} (\beta_{12})^\frac{5}{2} + \alpha_{2}g_{2} + \overline{\alpha_{2}} \overline{g_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{5}{2}   \right] \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \left[ \alpha_{1}g_{1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \overline{\alpha_{2}} \overline{g_{2}} \right] \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \alpha_{1}g_{1} \int^{x_{1}}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_{1}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
Substituting all terms and evaluating 
\begin{equation}
\begin{split}
    K.E &= C\Biggl[\frac{4}{7} \biggl[ \alpha_{1}g_{1} \biggl[ (\beta_{12})^\frac{5}{2} - (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \biggr] + \alpha_{2}g_{2}   \biggr] \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} +   \frac{4}{7} \overline{\alpha_{2}} \overline{g_{2}} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{4}{7} \alpha_{1} g_{1} \sqrt{x} (f_{1}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{1}} \\ & \quad + \frac{5 N_{2}}{21 \eta}   \biggl[ \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^{2} \frac{Q_{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ \alpha_{1} g_{1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \overline{\alpha_{2}} \overline{g_{2}} \biggr] -\alpha_{1} g_{1} (\beta_{12})^\frac{5}{2} - \overline{\alpha_{2}} \overline{g_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{5}{2} - \alpha_{2} g_{2} \biggr] \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{5}{21 \eta}  \biggl[ \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^{2}}{Q_{1}} \alpha_{1}g_{1} \biggl[ \overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}} \\ & \quad + \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) N_{2}Q_{2} - (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{\frac{3}{2}} N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}} \biggr] - \biggl[ \alpha_{1} g_{1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \overline{\alpha_{2}} \overline{g_{2}} \biggr] \biggl[ \overline{N_{2}} + \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} - (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} N_{2} \biggr]   \biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{5 \alpha_{1}g_{1}}{21 \eta} \biggl[ N_{1} \\ & \quad + \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{Q_{1}} + (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^{\frac{3}{2}} \biggl[ (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} N_{2} - \overline{N_{2}} \biggr]  - (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} N_{2} - (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2} \overline{Q_{2}}}{Q_{1}} + \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{Q_{1}}   \biggr] \frac{df_{1}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{1}}\Biggr]
    \end{split}
\end{equation}
    where, 
    \begin{equation*}
        C = \frac{12\hbar^2\sqrt{R}}{20 \pi m_1}\biggl[\frac{2\times(4/3)\alpha_s}{a}\biggr]^\frac{5}{2}
    \end{equation*}
\subsection{Calculation of Potential Energy}
The potential energy of Case II also depends on seven different constants. The analytical calculation of each of these constants is derived below.  
 \subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{11}$}
 \begin{equation}
     K_{11} =\int^{x_1}_0 dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_1}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
     \label{92}
 \end{equation}
  \begin{equation}
                    K_{11} = K_{11} \cdot 1 + K_{11} \cdot 2 
                    \label{93}
                \end{equation}
At first,
 \begin{equation}
     K_{11} \cdot 1 = \int^{x_1}_0 dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}
     \label{94}
 \end{equation}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}\int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
 \begin{split}
     &= (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr] \biggr] + (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[\frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \\ & \quad + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}}  \biggl[x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr] \Biggr] + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} - \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \gamma_{1\overline{2}}  + \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr] \Biggr] 
     \end{split}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
 \begin{split}
     &= \frac{(\beta_{12})^3}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - \frac{(\beta_{12})^3}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} - \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \gamma_{1\overline{2}} + \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
     \end{split}
 \end{equation*} 
 \begin{equation*}
 \begin{split}
     &= \frac{(\beta_{12})^3}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \\ & \quad - \frac{(\beta_{12})^3}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \Biggr] \\ & \quad \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} - \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \gamma_{1\overline{2}} + \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \Biggr] \\ & \quad \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
     \end{split}
 \end{equation*}
 The first, second, and third terms get canceled with $K_{11} \cdot 2$,
 \begin{equation}
 \begin{split}
     &= - \frac{(\beta_{12})^3}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \Biggr] \\ & \quad \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} - \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1}\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \gamma_{1\overline{2}} + \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \Biggr] \\ & \quad \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
     \end{split}
     \label{95}
 \end{equation}
 For convenience, the above equation is evaluated in three separate sections.
 \begin{itemize}
     \item Section 1
 \end{itemize}
 \begin{equation}
   - \frac{(\beta_{12})^3}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
   \label{96}
\end{equation}
Substituting values of the above integration from the appendix
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
    &=     - \frac{(\beta_{12})^3}{Q_2} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggr] -   \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{4}{7} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{5}{7\overline{Q_{2}}} \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta} \\ & \quad + \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) -  \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{5}{7\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggr] -   \frac{1}{Q_1} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_1}  \\ & \quad -   \frac{4}{7} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{5}{7Q_1} \Biggl[ \frac{N_1 Q_1}{3 \eta} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} - \frac{\overline{N_{2}} Q_1}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{N_2 Q_1}{3 \eta} \bigl[ (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} - (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \bigr] \\ & \quad - \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} +   \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \Biggr] \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  + \frac{5}{7 Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2 \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta} + \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad -   \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1 \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +  \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3  \\ & \quad   \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1 \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}  \\ & \quad +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1 \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} +   \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  \\ & \quad +   \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1 \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  \\ & \quad +   \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} +   \frac{N_1}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} +   \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \biggl[ (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} -   (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \biggr] \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  -   \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} +   \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} 
        \end{split}
    \end{equation*}
    \begin{equation}
    \begin{split}
        &=   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} \frac{(\beta_{12})^3}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -    \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -    \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad +  \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{N_1}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} 
        \end{split}
        \label{104}
    \end{equation}
      Therefore, $K_{11} = K_{11} \cdot 1 + K_{11} \cdot 2$
      \begin{equation*}
      \begin{split}
          &=   \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7 Q_2} (\beta_{12})^3 \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} +  \frac{4}{7 Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{4}{7\overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad -   \frac{4}{7 Q_1} \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_1} +   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{12})^3 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2}\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} + \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} \frac{(\beta_{12})^3}{Q_2} - \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)+ \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 \frac{Q_2}{{\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} -    \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \Biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{5 \overline{N_{2}}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +  \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3  \\ & \quad   \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}  -   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -  \frac{5 \overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}} }{21 \eta {Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2 \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta {Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2 \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta} \frac{\overline{Q_{2}}}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2 (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 - \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \\ & \quad - \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) - \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} - \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \\ & \quad + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} \frac{\overline{Q_{2}}}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2 + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} \frac{Q_2}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) -   \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2 \Biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{5 N_1}{21 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 \overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{21 \eta {Q_1}^2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{5 \overline{N_{2}}}{21 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} +   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad -   \frac{5 N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{21 \eta {Q_1}^2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} +   \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta {Q_1}^2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} + \Biggl[ \frac{N_1}{3 \eta Q_1} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \\ & \quad - \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \gamma_{1\overline{2}} +   \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \Biggr] \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}
          \end{split}
      \end{equation*} 
      \begin{equation}
    \begin{split}
         K_{11} & = \frac{4}{7} \biggl[ \frac{\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_{2}}} -   \frac{(\beta_{12})^3}{Q_2} \biggr] \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{4}{7} \biggl[\frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2}}{Q_1} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggr] \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -  \frac{4}{7 Q_1} \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad + \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{12})^3}{Q_2}  - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} + \frac{Q_2}{{\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \Biggr] + \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{12})^3}{Q_2} \\ & \quad -   \frac{Q_2}{{\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \Biggr] \Biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  + \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}}}{Q_1} - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_{2}}} + \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2}}{Q_1} \\ & \quad + \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} - \frac{Q_2}{{\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}}}{Q_1}  + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_1} + \frac{Q_2}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2 \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) - \frac{\overline{Q_{2}}}{{Q_1}^2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2 \Biggr] \\ & \quad + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3}{\overline{Q_{2}}} - \frac{2(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2}}{Q_1} + \frac{\overline{Q_{2}}}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2 \Biggr]  + \frac{5}{21 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 + \frac{N_2 Q_2}{{\overline{Q_{2}}}^2}(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) - \frac{N_2}{\overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^3 \\ & \quad - \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2 - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2 \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)  +   \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{{Q_1}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2 (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] \Biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \Biggl[ \frac{N_1}{3 \eta Q_1} - \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta {Q_1}^2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta Q_{1}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \Biggl[ (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{Q_2}{Q_1} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \gamma_{1\overline{2}}  + \frac{\overline{Q_{2}}}{Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \Biggr] + \frac{5}{21 \eta} \Biggl[ \frac{N_1}{Q_1} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{{Q_1}^2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} - \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad + \frac{N_2}{Q_1} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2}{Q_1} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} -   \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{{Q_1}^2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}}  + \frac{N_2 Q_2}{{Q_1}^2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \Biggr] \Biggr] \frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_1} 
          \end{split}
          \label{105}
      \end{equation} 
\subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{22}$} 
 \begin{equation}
     K_{22} = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}dx \int^{x}_{0} \sqrt{x^\prime}(f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}dx^{\prime} + \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}dx \int^{x_{2}}_{x} \frac{(f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}} dx^\prime
 \end{equation}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \biggl. \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} dx \biggl[ \frac{1}{Q_{2}}\biggl(x^\prime \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^{\prime}} - f_{2}(x^\prime) \biggr)\biggr|_{x}\biggr] + \int^{x_{2}}_{0}\sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} dx \frac{1}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{x} \frac{d^{2}f_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^{\prime^2}}dx^{\prime}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}dx \cdot \frac{1}{Q_{2}}\biggl( x^\prime\frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime} - f_{2}(x^\prime)   \biggr) \Bigg|_{x}+ \frac{1}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} dx \biggl( \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x_{2}} - \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^{\prime}}\biggr|_{x}\biggr)
 \end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
   = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} dx \cdot \frac{1}{Q_{2}}\biggl( x^{\prime} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^{\prime}} - f_{2}(x^\prime)\biggr)\biggr|_{x} + \frac{1}{Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^{\prime}}\biggr|_{x_{2}} \cdot\frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}  - \frac{1}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^{\prime}} \biggr|_{x} dx
   \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
