    = \frac{1}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} - \frac{1}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = - \frac{1}{Q_{2}}\int^{x_{2}}_{0}\frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}dx + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} \frac{1}{Q_{2}}\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\biggr|_{x_{2}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = - \frac{1}{Q_{2}}   \biggl[ \frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{5 N_{2}}{21 \eta} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{x}\biggr|_{x_{2}}\biggr] + \frac{1}{Q_{2}} \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \biggr|_{x_{2}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
   K_{22} = -   \frac{4}{7Q_{2}} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{4 N_{2}}{7 \eta Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\biggr|_{x_{2}}
\end{equation}
\subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{\overline{2}\overline{2}}$}
\begin{equation}
    K_{\overline{2}\overline{2}} = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0}dx' \sqrt{x'} (\overline{f_{2}(x')})^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} dx' \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x')})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation}
\begin{equation*}
    K_{\overline{2}\overline{2}} = K_{\overline{2}\overline{2}} \cdot 1 + K_{\overline{2} \overline{2}} \cdot 2 
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    K_{\overline{2}\overline{2}} \cdot 1 = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0} dx' \sqrt{x'} (\overline{f_{2}(x')})^\frac{3}{2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
   =  \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0} dx' \sqrt{x'}(\overline{f_{2}(x')})^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0} dx' \sqrt{x'} (\overline{f_{2}(x')})^\frac{3}{2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
   =  \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \left[ \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \biggl[ x \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} - f_{2}(x) \biggr] \right] + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \left[ \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta\overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) + \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr] \right]
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
\begin{split}
   & =  \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad - \frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}  + \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
    \end{split}
\end{equation}
\begin{equation}
    K_{\overline{2} \overline{2}} \cdot 2 = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} dx' \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x')})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
\end{equation}
\begin{equation*}
    = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx' \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} dx' \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x')})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
    & = \int^{x_{2}}_{0}dx\sqrt{x}(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}\left[ \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} + \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \right] + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad \biggl[ \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \biggr]
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
    &= \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}  \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} + \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad - \frac{\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
\begin{split}
    & = \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0}dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} + \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad - \frac{\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} 
    \end{split}
\end{equation}
Adding $K_{\overline{2}\overline{2}} \cdot 1$ + $K_{\overline{2}\overline{2}} \cdot 2$
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
    & = - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}  \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}}   + \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}
    \end{split}
    \end{equation*}
    \begin{equation*}
    \begin{split}
     & = - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}}  - \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad + \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta Q_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad - \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}  \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}
        \end{split}
    \end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
   &= - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}  - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)  \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad + \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta Q_{2} } (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2}}{ 3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{\frac{3}{2}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \\ & \quad  + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    \begin{split}
        & = - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}}{Q_{2}} \biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x}   (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{5 N_{2}}{21 \eta}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \biggr] - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[   \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{5}{21 \eta } \biggl[ \overline{N_{2}} \\ & \quad - N_{2}(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{\frac{3}{2}}  + \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \beta_{\overline{2}2}  \biggr]   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \frac{5}{21 \eta} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^{2} \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \biggr] - \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \\ & \quad - \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2} }{3\eta Q_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{\frac{3}{2}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad  - \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{\frac{3}{2}}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    \begin{split}
        &=   - \frac{4}{7 Q_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} +  \frac{5 N_{2}}{21 \eta Q_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} -  \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_{2}}} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^{\frac{5}{2}} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}}  \\ & \quad + \frac{5 \overline{N_{2}}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{5 N_{2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \frac{5 N_{2} Q_{2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{5 N_{2} Q_{2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \\ & \quad - \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta Q_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \\ & \quad  + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} 
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
    \begin{split}
       K_{\overline{2}\overline{2}} &=    \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} -   \frac{4}{7 Q_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} -   \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_{2}}} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \frac{5 N_{2}}{21 \eta Q_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}}  \\ & \quad - \frac{5N_{2} Q_{2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta Q_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{3}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \\ & \quad - \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{5 \overline{N_{2}}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{5 N_{2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \frac{5 N_{2} Q_{2}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad - \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}^{2}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}   + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}  
    \end{split}
    \end{equation}
\subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{12}$}
\begin{equation}
    K_{12} = \int^{x_{1}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2})}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0} dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_{1}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{x}dx^\prime \frac{(f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}}
\end{equation}
\[ = K_{12}\cdot1 + K_{12} \cdot 2 \] 
\begin{equation}
    K_{12}\cdot 1 = \int^{x_{1}}_{0}dx \frac{(f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0}dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}
\end{equation}
\begin{equation}
\begin{split}
   & = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0} dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx \frac{(f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0} dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad + \int^{x_{1}}_{\overline{x_{2}}}dx \frac{(f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0}dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}
    \end{split}
\end{equation}
\subsubsection*{Taking 1\textsuperscript{st} term}
\begin{equation*}
    \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}\int^{x}_{0} dx^{\prime} \sqrt{x^{\prime}} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    =(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0}dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \bigl( x^\prime \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime} - f_{2}(x^\prime)\biggr)\biggr|_{x}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
    =\frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}}\int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x} - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}}\int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation}
\subsubsection*{Taking 2\textsuperscript{nd} term}
\begin{equation}
   = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0} dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2} \qquad for \qquad [x_{2}<x<\overline{x_{2}}]
\end{equation}
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
   & = (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \biggl[ \underbrace{\int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}_{\text{Using NC}}+ \underbrace{\int^{x}_{x_{2}} dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}\biggr]}_{[f_{2}(x) = 0 ]}  
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\subsubsection*{Taking 3\textsuperscript{rd} term}
\begin{equation}
    \int^{x_{1}}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}\int^{x}_{0} dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}
\end{equation}
\begin{equation*}
    =\int^{x_{1}}_{\overline{x_{2}}}dx \frac{(f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \biggl[ \underbrace{\int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}_{\text{Using NC}} + \underbrace{\int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime}(f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}_{[f_{2}(x) = 0]} + \underbrace{ \int^{x}_{\overline{x_{2}}}dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}\biggr]}_{[f_{2}(x) = 0]}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} \int^{x_{1}}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} \frac{1}{Q_{1}} \biggl[ \frac{df_{1}(x)}{dx}\biggr|_{x_{1}} - \frac{df_{1}(x)}{dx}\biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}}\biggr]
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    K_{12}\cdot2 = \int^{x_{1}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{x} dx^\prime \frac{(f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
    & = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{x} dx^\prime \frac{(f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}} + \underbrace{\int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx \sqrt{x} (f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}_{[x_{2}<x<\overline{x_{2}}]} \underbrace{\int^{x_{2}}_{x} dx^\prime \frac{(f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}}}_{[f_{2}(x) =0]} \\ & \quad + \underbrace{ \int^{x_{1}}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}_{[\overline{x_{2}}<x<x_{1}]} \underbrace{\int^{x_{2}}_{x} dx^\prime \frac{(f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}}}_{[f_{2}(x) = 0 ]}
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime} \biggr|_{x_{2}} - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x}
\end{equation*}
\text{On adding 1\textsuperscript{st}, 2\textsuperscript{nd}, 3\textsuperscript{rd} term and $K_{12}\cdot2$}
\begin{equation}
\begin{split}
     & = \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}}\int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime} \biggr|_{x} - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}}\int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}\int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x))})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} \frac{1}{Q_{1}} \biggl[ \frac{df_{1}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x_{1}} - \frac{df_{1}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \biggr] + \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x_{2}}\int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\biggr|_{x}
    \end{split}
 \end{equation}
 \begin{equation*}
 \begin{split}
      & = -\frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}}\int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}\frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}\frac{1}{Q_{1}}\biggl[ \frac{df_{1}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x_{1}} - \frac{df_{1}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}}\biggr] \\ & \quad + \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}}\frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x_{2}}\int^{x_{2}}_{0}dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}
     \end{split}
 \end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
  & = - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}}   \biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{5}{7Q_{2}}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \cdot \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta} \biggr] + (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} \biggl[ \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}}  \biggr] \\ & \quad  + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta Q_{1}}   \biggl[ \frac{df_{1}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{1}} -   \frac{df_{1}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \biggr] + \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \cdot \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}}
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
\begin{split}
  K_{12}  &= -   \frac{4}{7 Q_{2}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} \biggl[ \frac{5}{7 Q_{2}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggr]   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \\ & \quad + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} \biggl[ \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} - \frac{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}{Q_{1}}  \biggr]   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta Q_{1}}   \frac{df_{1}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{1}}
  \end{split}
\end{equation}
\subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{21}$}
\begin{equation}
    K_{21} = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0}dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{1}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_{2}}_{0}dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{1}}_{x} dx^\prime \frac{(f_{1}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}}
\end{equation}
\begin{equation}
    = K_{21}\cdot1 + K_{21} \cdot 2 
\end{equation}
\begin{equation}
    K_{21} \cdot 1 = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0}dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{1}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}
\end{equation}
\begin{equation*}
    = (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{0}dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0} dx^\prime \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \biggl[ x^\prime    \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime} - f_{2}(x^\prime)\biggr] \Bigg|_{x}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
    = \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x} - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}}\int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation} 
\begin{equation}
    K_{21} \cdot 2 = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{1}}_{x} dx^\prime \frac{(f_{1}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}}
\end{equation}
\begin{equation*}
     = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \biggl[ \int^{x_{2}}_{x} dx^\prime \frac{(f_{1}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}}    + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx^\prime \frac{(f_{1}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}} + \int^{x_{1}}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx^\prime \frac{(f_{1}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}}  \biggr]
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
  & = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x_{2}} - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^\prime} \biggr|_{x_{2}} \\ & \quad + \frac{1}{Q_{1}} \frac{df_{1}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime} \biggr|_{x_{1}} - \frac{1}{Q_{1}} \frac{df_{1}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime} \biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}}     \biggr]
   \end{split}
   \end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
\begin{split}
   & = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x}(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x_{2}} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^\prime} \biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^\prime} \biggr|_{x_{2}} + \frac{1}{Q_{1}} \frac{df_{1}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime} \biggr|_{x_{1}} \\ & \quad - \frac{1}{Q_{1}} \frac{df_{1}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime} \biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \biggr]   - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x} \sqrt{x}(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}dx 
    \end{split}
\end{equation}
Finally on adding 
\begin{equation}
    K_{21} = K_{21}\cdot 1 + K_{21} \cdot 2
\end{equation}
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
   &  = - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0}dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \biggl[  \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime} \biggr|_{x_{2}} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^\prime} \biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x^\prime)}}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x_{2}} \\ & \quad + \frac{1}{Q_{1}} \frac{df_{1}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime}\biggr|_{x_{1}} - \frac{1}{Q_{1}} \frac{df_{1}(x^\prime)}{dx^\prime} \biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}}\biggr]\frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    \begin{split}
        & = - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}}    \biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{5N_{2}}{21 \eta Q_{2}}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \biggr] + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \biggl[ \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \biggr] \\ & \quad + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta Q_{1}}   \biggl[ \frac{df_{1}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{1}} -   \frac{df_{1}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \biggr] + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta Q_{2}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} 
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
\begin{split}
  K_{21}  &= -   \frac{4}{7 Q_{2}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} \biggl[ \frac{5}{7 Q_{2}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggr]   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \\ & \quad + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} \biggl[ \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} - \frac{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}{Q_{1}}  \biggr]   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta Q_{1}}   \frac{df_{1}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{1}}
  \end{split}
\end{equation}
\subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{\overline{2}2}$}
\begin{equation}
    K_{\overline{2}2} = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}dx \int^{x}_{0}\sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2} dx^\prime + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}dx \int^{x_{2}}_{x} \frac{(f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}}dx^\prime
\end{equation}
\begin{equation}
    = K_{\overline{2}2} \cdot 1 + K_{\overline{2}2} \cdot 2 
\end{equation}
\begin{equation}
    K_{\overline{2}2} \cdot 1 = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} dx \int^{x}_{0} \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2} dx^\prime
\end{equation}
\begin{equation*}
     = \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} dx \int^{x}_{0} \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2} dx^\prime + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} dx \int^{x}_{0} \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2} dx^\prime
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
    & = (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}dx \int^{x}_{0} \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2} dx + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} dx  \biggl[ \underbrace{\int^{x_{2}}_{0} \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2} dx^\prime }_{\text{Using NC}} \\ & \quad + \underbrace{\int^{x}_{x_{2}} \sqrt{x^\prime} (f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2} dx^\prime}_{[f_{2}(x) = 0 ]} \biggr] 
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
     = \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}dx \biggl( x\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} - f_{2}(x) \biggr) \biggr|_{x} + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} \biggl[ \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\biggr|_{x_{2}} \biggr]
\end{equation}
\begin{equation}
    K_{\overline{2}2} \cdot 2 = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0}\sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} dx \int^{x_{2}}_{x}\frac{(f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}} dx^\prime
\end{equation}
\begin{equation*}
     = \int^{x_{2}}_{0}\sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} dx \int^{x_{2}}_{x} \frac{(f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}}dx^\prime+ \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} dx \underbrace{\biggl( - \int^{x}_{x_{2}} \frac{(f_{2}(x^\prime))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x^\prime}} dx^\prime\biggr)}_{[f_{2}(x) = 0]} 
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}  \biggl( \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \biggr|_{x_{2}} - \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \biggr|_{x} \biggr)
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
     = \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx  \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \biggr|_{x_{2}} - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}
\end{equation}
Finally, 
\begin{equation}
    K_{\overline{2}2} = K_{\overline{2}2} \cdot 1 + K_{\overline{2}2} \cdot 2 
\end{equation}
\begin{equation*}
\begin{split}
    &= \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}dx \biggl( x \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} -f_{2}(x) \biggr) \biggr|_{x} + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} \biggl[ \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \biggr|_{x_{2}} \biggr]  \\ & \quad + \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} dx \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \biggr|_{x_{2}}  - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}
    \end{split}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{0} \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} dx + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \biggr|_{x_{2}} \biggr] + \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \biggr|_{x_{2}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \biggl[  \frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x_{2}} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} - \frac{5N_{2}}{21 \eta} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \biggr|_{x_{2}}  \biggr] + \biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} - \frac{\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggr] \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \biggr|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \biggr|_{\overline{x_{2}}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
  K_{\overline{2}2}  = -    \frac{4}{7 Q_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} \biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} - \frac{\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} + \frac{5}{7Q_{2}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \biggr]   \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} + \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}}   \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}
\end{equation}
                \subsubsection{Calculation of $K_{\overline{2}1}$}
                \begin{equation}
                    K_{\overline{2}1} = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{0} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
                    \label{82}
                \end{equation}
                \begin{equation}
                    K_{\overline{2}1} = K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 1 + K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 2 
                    \label{83}
                \end{equation}
                At first, 
                \begin{equation}
                    K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 1 = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}
                    \label{84}
                \end{equation}
                \begin{equation*}
                    = \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0} dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \int^{x}_{0} dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} 
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                 = (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl( x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr) \Biggr] + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[ \int^{x_2}_0 dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x}_{x_2} dx' \sqrt{x'} (f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr]
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    &= \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \Biggl[\int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \cdot x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggr] + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \Biggl[ \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad  + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl( x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr) - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \Biggr] 
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                &= \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \\ & \quad + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
                \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                The first term and fourth term get canceled with $K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 2$
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    & = \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \\ & \quad \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    &= \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2} -  \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2}  -   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggr] -   \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad -   \frac{4}{7} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{5}{7\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \Biggl[\frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta} + \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) - \frac{N_2 \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] \\ & \quad +  \frac{5}{7\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta}\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggr] - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2} 
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    &=  \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7 Q_2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} +  \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad   \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  -   \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \Biggl[ \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{4}{7} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{5\overline{N_{2}}}{21 \eta} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -  \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad +  \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \Biggr] -   \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad +  \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    &=  \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7 Q_2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  -  \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{4}{7\overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +  \frac{5 \overline{N_{2}}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}  -  \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad -   \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}  +  \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation}
                \begin{split}
                    & =   \frac{4}{7\overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7 Q_2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -    \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +  \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +  \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +  \frac{5 \overline{N_{2}}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}  \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}  -  \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation}
                At second,
                \begin{equation}
                    K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 2 = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
                    \label{86}
                \end{equation}
                \begin{equation*}
                    = \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    &= \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \int^{x_2}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} \Biggr] + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad \Biggl[ \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}}  + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} \Biggr]
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    &= (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx' \frac{(\overline{f_2(x')})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} \Biggr] \\ & \quad + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}  \Biggl[ (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} dx' \frac{(\overline{f_2(x')})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx' \frac{(f_1(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} \Biggr]
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    &= (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[\frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx' \frac{d^2f_2(x')}{dx'^2} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx' \frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x')}}{dx'^2} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx' \frac{d^2f_1(x')}{dx'^2} \Biggr] \\ & \quad + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} dx' \frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x')}}{dx'^2} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx' \frac{d^2f_1(x')}{dx'^2} \biggr] 
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    &= (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[   \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2}\frac{df_2(x)}{dx} +   \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  \\ & \quad +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}{Q_1} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \Biggr] + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[   \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  \\ & \quad -   \frac{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}{Q_1} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \Biggr]
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    & =  \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \\ & \quad +   \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +   \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_1} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x}(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}}}{Q_1} \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}{Q_1} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                The second term and eighth term get canceled with $K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 1$
                \begin{equation*}
                \begin{split}
                    &=  \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1}(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \\ & \quad   \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}  -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} \\ & \quad -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -   \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation}
                    = \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} -   \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \Biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \biggl[ \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{1}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} -   \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}
                    \label{87}
                \end{equation}
                Therefore, $K_{\overline{2}1} = K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 1 + K_{\overline{2}1} \cdot 2$
                \begin{equation}
                \begin{split}
                  K_{\overline{2}1}  & =   \frac{4}{7\overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7 Q_2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{4}{7 \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta Q_2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  -   \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -    \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad +  \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2 \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} +  \frac{N_2}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +  \frac{5 \overline{N_{2}}}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}  \\ & \quad +   \frac{5 N_2 Q_2}{21 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}  -  \frac{5 N_2}{21 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta {\overline{Q_{2}}}^2} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}  \\ & \quad + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggl[ \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} -   \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left( \frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \Biggr] \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2}  + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \biggl[ \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{1}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} -   \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggr] \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} +   \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1}
                    \end{split}
                \end{equation}
    \section{Appendices}
            The appendices for Case I are included in Appendix I and Case II is included in Appendix II. 
            \subsection{Appendix I}
1.    Normalization condition gives
    \begin{equation*}
      \int^{\overline{x_{max}}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_I(x)})^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_I}}{3\eta} 
      \label{33}
    \end{equation*}
    \begin{equation}
        \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}
    \end{equation}
2.    Normalization condition gives
    \begin{equation*}
      \int^{x_{max}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_I(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_I}{3\eta} 
      \label{33}
    \end{equation*}
    \begin{equation}
        \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_2}{3\eta}
    \end{equation}
   Similarly, \\
 3.   \begin{equation}
        \int^{x_1}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta}
    \end{equation}
  4. Normalization condition gives 
                \begin{equation*}
                \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta}    
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                    \frac{1}{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}\int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation}
                   \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}
                   \label{79}
                \end{equation}
    5. Normalization condition gives 
 \begin{equation*}
     \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} 
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     \frac{1}{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation}
     \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}
     \label{90}
 \end{equation}
 6.  \begin{equation*}
     \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{x}{Q_2} \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
      = \frac{1}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx x \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]_{x_2} - \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)} \biggr]_{\overline{x_2}}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[ \frac{N_2Q_2}{3 \eta} + \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) - \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \biggr] - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) 
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} + \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation}
     = \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}
     \label{91}
 \end{equation}        
 7.              \begin{equation*}
                \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} 
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                = \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{x}{Q_2} \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2} 
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                = \frac{1}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx x \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                = \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                    = \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]_{x_2} - \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]_{\overline{x_2}}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation}
                    = \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]_{x_2} - \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \Biggl[x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)} \Biggr]_{\overline{x_2}}
                \end{equation}
    8.            \begin{equation*}
               \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} 
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation*}
               = \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{x}{Q_1} \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2} 
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation*}
               = \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx x \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2} 
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation*}
               = \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]^{x_1}_{x_2} 
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation}
               = \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]_{x_1} - \frac{\gamma_{12}}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]_{x_2}
               \label{114}
           \end{equation}
9.          The Thomas - Fermi equation gives 
    \begin{equation*}
        \frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx^2} = \overline{Q_2} \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
    \end{equation*}
    \begin{equation*}
        x\frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx^2} = \overline{Q_2}\sqrt{x}(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}
    \end{equation*}
    \begin{equation*}
        \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 x \frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx^2} = \overline{Q_2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}
    \end{equation*}
    \begin{equation*}
        \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 x\frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx^2} = \frac{\overline{Q_2}\overline{N_2}}{3\eta} \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation}
            \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}- \overline{f_2(x)} \biggr]_{\overline{x_2}} = \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}
            \label{34}
        \end{equation}
10.        Normalization condition gives
        \begin{equation*}
            \int^{x_{max}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_I(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_I}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \int^{x_2}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_2}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x}(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}}dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_2}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x}(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_2}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}}dx \frac{x}{Q_2} \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2} = \frac{N_2}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \frac{1}{Q_2}\int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}}dx x \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2} = \frac{N_2}{3\eta} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x)\biggr]^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} = \frac{N_2}{3\eta} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}-f_2(x)\biggr]_{x_2} - \biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}-f_2(x)\biggr]_{\overline{x_2}} = \frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x)\biggr]_{x_2} - \beta_{2\overline{2}}\biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)}\biggr]_{\overline{x_2}} = \frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} -\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}-f_2(x)\biggr]_{x_2} - \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} = \frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta}-(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation}
            \biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}- f_2(x)\biggr]_{x_2} = \frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta}+ \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}
            \label{35}
        \end{equation}
11.        Normalization condition gives
        \begin{equation*}
            \int^{x_1}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}}dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta}        \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} + (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta} + (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{x}{Q_2}\frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2}+ \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx x \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x}(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
               \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2}\biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}- f_2(x)\biggr]^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{x}{Q_1}\frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[\biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x)\biggr]_{x_2} - \biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}-f_2(x)\biggr]_{\overline{x_2}}\biggr]+ \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2}dx x \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
              \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2}\biggl[\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} + \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}-\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\biggl(x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}-\overline{f_2(x)}\biggr)_{\overline{x_{2}}}\biggr]+ \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx x \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} + \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta} - \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}\biggr] + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x)\biggr]^{x_1}_{x_2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}\biggr] + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]^{x_1}_{x_2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]^{x_1}_{x_2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}-\frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2}\cdot\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta}+\frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2}\cdot(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \frac{1}{Q_1}\bigg[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]^{x_1}_{x_2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}-(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_2}{3\eta}+(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_1}-\biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_2} = \frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta}-(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta}-(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_2Q_1}{3\eta} + (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_1} - \gamma_{12}\biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}-f_2(x)\biggr]_{x_2} = \frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta}-(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta}-(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_2Q_1}{3\eta}+(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_1} - \gamma_{12}\biggl[\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta}+ \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}-(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}\biggr] = \frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta}- (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta}-(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_2Q_1}{3\eta}+(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
                \biggl[ x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_1} &= \frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta}- (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta}-(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_2Q_1}{3\eta}+ (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^{\frac{3}{2}} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta} + \gamma_{12}\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} + \gamma_{12}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} \\ & \quad - \gamma_{12}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}
                \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
                \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_1} & = \frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta}+\gamma_{12}\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta}-(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_2Q_1}{3\eta}+\biggl[(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}-(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\biggr]\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta}-\gamma_{12}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta} \\ & \quad +\gamma_{12}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}
                \label{36}
                \end{split}
            \end{equation}
 12.
                      \begin{equation*}
                         \int dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} = \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} - \frac{5}{7\overline{Q_2}}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}-\overline{f_2(x)}\biggr]
                      \end{equation*}
                      \begin{equation*}
                          \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} =  \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{5}{7\overline{Q_2}}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}-\overline{f_2(x)}\biggr]_{\overline{x_2}}
                      \end{equation*}
                      From eqn \ref{34},
                      \begin{equation*}
                            \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} =  \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{5}{7\overline{Q_2}}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}
                      \end{equation*}
                      \begin{equation}
                          \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} =   \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -   \frac{5\overline{N_2}}{21\eta}\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}
                          \label{38}
                      \end{equation}
13.
                      \begin{equation*}
                          \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}}dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}=  \frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x}(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{5}{7Q_2}\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}}\biggl[x\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}-f_2(x)\biggr]^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}}
                      \end{equation*}
                      From eqns \ref{34} and \ref{35},
                      \begin{equation}
                       \begin{split} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} &=   \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{4}{7} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} -  \frac{5}{7Q_2}\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}\biggl[\frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} + \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} \\ & \quad 
 - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}\biggr]  +   \frac{5}{7Q_2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)^2\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}
                          \label{39}
                          \end{split}
                      \end{equation}
14.
                      \begin{equation*}
                          \int^{x_1}_{x_2}dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}=  \frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x}(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|^{x_1}_{x_2} -  \frac{5}{7Q_1}\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|^{x_1}_{x_2}\biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]^{x_1}_{x_2}
                      \end{equation*}
                      \begin{equation*}
