                          = \frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x}(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_1} -  \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2} -  \frac{5}{7Q_1}\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_1} \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_1} +  \frac{5}{7Q_1}\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2}\biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_2} 
                      \end{equation*}
                      \begin{equation}                  \begin{split}   \int^{x_1}_{x_2}dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} &= \frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x}(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_1} -  \frac{4}{7}(\gamma_{12})^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_2}- \frac{5}{7Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_1}\Biggl[\frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta} + \gamma_{12} \frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta} - (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_2Q_1}{3\eta} \\ & \quad  +\biggl[(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\biggr] \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_1}{3\eta} - \gamma_{12}(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}   + \gamma_{12}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta}\Biggr] +  \frac{5}{7Q_1}(\gamma_{12})^2
\frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} \\ & \quad \biggl[ \frac{N_2Q_2}{3\eta}  + \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)\frac{\overline{N_2}\overline{Q_2}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{\overline{N_2}Q_2}{3\eta}\biggr]
\end{split}
\label{40}
\end{equation}
15.
\begin{equation*}
    \frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx^2} = \overline{Q_2} \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    \frac{1}{\overline{Q_2}}\frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx^2} = \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{1}{\overline{Q_2}}\frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx^2} = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
    \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} =  \frac{1}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}
    \label{44}
\end{equation}
 16. 
                \begin{equation*}
                    \int^{x}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}
                \end{equation*}
                In region $0<x\leq \overline{x_2}$, 
                \begin{equation}
                    = \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \Biggl[x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)} \Biggr]
                    \label{78}
                \end{equation}
17. 
\begin{equation*}
   \int^{x}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
   = \int^{x}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{x}{Q_2} \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{1}{Q_2} \int^{x}_{\overline{x_2}} dx x \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]^{x}_{\overline{x_2}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr] - \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)} \biggr]_{\overline{x_2}} 
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
  = \frac{1}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr] - \frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta Q_2}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)
    \label{56}
\end{equation}
 18. 
                \begin{equation}
                    \begin{split}
                        \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} & = \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \biggl( - \int^{x}_{x_2} dx' \frac{(f_2(x'))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x'}} \Biggr) \\ & = 0
                    \end{split}
                    \label{76}
                \end{equation}
                This is because $f_2(x)$ does not exist in region $> x_2$ \\
19. 
\begin{equation*}
    \int^{x_2}_{x} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
In region $0<x\leq \overline{x_2}$,
\begin{equation*}
    = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_{x} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_{x} dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_{x} dx \frac{1}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx^2} + \frac{1}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    =   \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|^{\overline{x_2}}_{x} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
    =   \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} - \frac{(\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} +   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} -   \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}}
    \label{57}
\end{equation}
20.
\begin{equation*}
    \int^{x_2}_{x} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
In region $\overline{x_2}<x \leq x_2$,
\begin{equation*}
    = \int^{x_2}_{x} dx \frac{1}{Q_2}\frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    =   \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|^{x_2}_{x}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
    =  \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{1}{Q_2} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}
    \label{58}
\end{equation}
    21. 
        \begin{equation*}
            \int^{x}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}
        \end{equation*}
        In region $0<x\leq\overline{x_2}$, 
        \begin{equation*}
           = (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            = (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x}_0 dx \frac{x}{\overline{Q_2}} \frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx^2} 
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            = \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{x}_0 dx x \frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx^2} 
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation}
            = \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \biggl[ x\frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)}\biggr]
            \label{106}
        \end{equation}
        22. 
         In region $\overline{x_2}<x\leq x_2$,  
         \begin{equation*}
             = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}
         \end{equation*}
         \begin{equation*}
             = (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} + (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} 
             \end{equation*}
             \begin{equation*}
                 = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x}_{\overline{x_2}} dx x \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2}
             \end{equation*}
             \begin{equation*}
                 = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]^{x}_{\overline{x_2}} 
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                    = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr] - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]_{\overline{x_2}}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                    = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr] - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right)}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_2(x)} \biggr]_{\overline{x_2}}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation}
                    = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]
                    \label{107}
                \end{equation}
                23. 
                 In region $x_2<x\leq x_1$, 
      \begin{equation*}
          = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
          = (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2} + (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x}_{x_2} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} 
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
          = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx x \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x}_{x_2} dx x \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
          = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx}- f_2(x) \biggr]^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]^{x}_{x_2}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
          = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]_{x_2} - \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]_{\overline{x_2}} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr] - \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]_{x_2}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
      \begin{split}
         &= \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \Biggl[ \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta} + \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) - \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr] \\ & \quad  - \frac{\gamma_{12}}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} - f_2(x) \biggr]_{x_2}
         \end{split}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
      \begin{split}
          &= \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_2} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12}  \\ & \quad - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \gamma_{12}  + \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12}  + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]
          \end{split}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation}
          = \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{N_2}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_2 Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{12} - \frac{\overline{N_2}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_2} \overline{Q_2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \gamma_{12} + \frac{\overline{N_2} Q_2}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{2\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{12} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]
          \label{108}
      \end{equation}
      24. 
      \begin{equation*}
          \int^{x_1}_{x} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
      \end{equation*}
      In region $0<x\leq\overline{x_2}$,
      \begin{equation*}
        = \int^{\overline{x_2}}_{x} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}   
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
          = (\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_{x} dx \frac{(\overline{f_2(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
          = \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \int^{\overline{x_2}}_{x} dx \frac{d^2\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx^2} + \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{\overline{x_2}} dx \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2} 
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation}
          =   \frac{(\beta_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_2}} \biggl[ \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} - \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \biggr] +   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[ \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} -   \left(\frac{\beta_{1\overline{2}}}{\gamma_{12}}\right) \frac{d\overline{f_2(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_2}} \biggr] +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -  \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \biggr]
          \label{109}
      \end{equation}
      25. 
           In region $\overline{x_2}<x\leq x_2$,
           \begin{equation*}
               = \int^{x_2}_{x} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation*}
               = (\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation*}
               = \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \int^{x_2}_{x} dx \frac{d^2f_2(x)}{dx^2} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x_2} dx \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2}
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation}
               =   \frac{(\gamma_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_2} \biggl[ \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} - \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \biggr] +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -  \gamma_{12} \frac{df_2(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_2} \biggr]
               \label{110}
           \end{equation}
           26. \\ 
           In region $x_2<x\leq x_1$,
           \begin{equation*}
               \int^{x_1}_{x} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation*}
               = \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2}
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation}
               =  \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} - \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \biggr]
               \label{111}
           \end{equation}
            \subsection{Appendix II}
1.    Normalization condition gives
    \begin{equation*}
      \int^{x_{max}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_I(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_I}{3\eta} 
      \label{33}
    \end{equation*}
    \begin{equation}
        \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}
    \end{equation}
2.    Normalization condition gives
    \begin{equation*}
      \int^{\overline{x_{max}}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_I(x)})^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_I}}{3\eta} 
      \label{33}
    \end{equation*}
    \begin{equation}
        \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta}
    \end{equation}
   Similarly, \\
 3.   \begin{equation}
        \int^{x_1}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_{1}(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta}
    \end{equation}
   4. Normalization condition gives 
                \begin{equation*}
                \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta}    
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                    \frac{1}{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}\int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation}
                   \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}
                   \label{79}
                \end{equation}
    5. Normalization condition gives 
 \begin{equation*}
     \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} 
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     \frac{1}{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation}
     \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}
     \label{90}
 \end{equation}
 6.  \begin{equation*}
     \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{x}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
      = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx x \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[x\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} - f_{2}(x) \biggr]_{x_{2}}
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta} + \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta} \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) - \frac{N_{2} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \biggr] - \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}}\left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) 
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation*}
     = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} + \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) - \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)
 \end{equation*}
 \begin{equation}
     = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} - \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}
     \label{91}
 \end{equation}        
 7.              \begin{equation*}
                \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} 
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{x}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2} 
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx x \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                    = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]_{x_{2}}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation}
                    = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \Biggl[x \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} - f_{2}(x) \Biggr]_{x_{2}}
                \end{equation}
    8.            \begin{equation*}
               \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} 
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation*}
               = \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{x}{Q_1} \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2} 
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation*}
               = \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx x \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2} 
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation*}
               = \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} 
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation}
               = \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]_{x_1} - \frac{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}}
               \label{114}
           \end{equation}
9.          The Thomas - Fermi equation gives 
    \begin{equation*}
        \frac{d^2f_{2}(x)}{dx^2} = Q_{2} \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
    \end{equation*}
    \begin{equation*}
        x\frac{d^2f_{2}(x)}{dx^2} = Q_{2}\sqrt{x}(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}
    \end{equation*}
    \begin{equation*}
        \int^{x_{2}}_0 x \frac{d^2f_{2}(x)}{dx^2} = Q_{2} \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}
    \end{equation*}
    \begin{equation*}
        \int^{x_{2}}_0 x\frac{d^2f_{2}(x)}{dx^2} = \frac{Q_{2}N_{2}}{3\eta} \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation}
            \biggl[ x \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}- f_{2}(x) \biggr]_{x_{2}} = \frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta}
            \label{84}
        \end{equation}
10.        Normalization condition gives
        \begin{equation*}
            \int^{x_{max}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_I(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_I}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x}(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x}(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx \frac{x}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2} = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx x \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2} = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)}\biggr]^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}-\overline{f_{2}(x)}\biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}-\overline{f_{2}(x)}\biggr]_{x_{2}} = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)}\biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \beta_{\overline{2}2}\biggl[x\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} - f_{2}(x)\biggr]_{x_{2}} = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta} -\left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}-\overline{f_{2}(x)}\biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta} = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}-(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation}
            \biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}- \overline{f_{2}(x)}\biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}} = \frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}+ \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}
            \label{85}
        \end{equation}
11.        Normalization condition gives
        \begin{equation*}
            \int^{x_1}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta}        \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} + (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}}{3\eta} + (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{x}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2}+ \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx x \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2} + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \sqrt{x}(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
               \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}- \overline{f_{2}(x)}\biggr]^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{x}{Q_1}\frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[\biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)}\biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}-\overline{f_{2}(x)}\biggr]_{x_{2}}\biggr]+ \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}}dx x \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
              \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\biggl[\frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta} + \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}-\left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\biggl(x\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}-f_{2}(x)\biggr)_{x_{2}}\biggr]+ \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx x \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[\frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta} + \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta} - \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta}\biggr] + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x)\biggr]^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[\frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}\biggr] + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}-\frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\cdot\frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}+\frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\cdot(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \frac{1}{Q_1}\bigg[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} = \frac{N_1}{3\eta} - (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}-(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3\eta}+(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_1}-\biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}} = \frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta}-(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}Q_1}{3\eta}-(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_{2}}Q_1}{3\eta} + (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}Q_1}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_1} - \gamma_{1\overline{2}}\biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}-\overline{f_{2}(x)}\biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}} = \frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta}-(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}Q_1}{3\eta}-(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_{2}}Q_1}{3\eta}+(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}Q_1}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
                \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_1} - \gamma_{1\overline{2}}\biggl[\frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}+ \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta}-(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}\biggr] = \frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta}- (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}Q_1}{3\eta}-(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_{2}}Q_1}{3\eta}+(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}Q_1}{3\eta}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation*}
            \begin{split}
                \biggl[ x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_1} &= \frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta}- (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}Q_1}{3\eta}-(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_{2}}Q_1}{3\eta}+ (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^{\frac{3}{2}} \frac{N_{2}Q_1}{3\eta} + \gamma_{1\overline{2}}\frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta} + \gamma_{1\overline{2}}\left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta} \\ & \quad - \gamma_{1\overline{2}}(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}
                \end{split}
            \end{equation*}
            \begin{equation}
            \begin{split}
                \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_1} & = \frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta}+\gamma_{1\overline{2}}\frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}-(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_{2}}Q_1}{3\eta}+\biggl[(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}-(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\biggr]\frac{N_{2}Q_1}{3\eta}-\gamma_{1\overline{2}}(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta} \\ & \quad +\gamma_{1\overline{2}}\left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta}
                \label{36}
                \end{split}
            \end{equation}
 12.
                      \begin{equation*}
                         \int dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} = \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} - \frac{5}{7Q_{2}}\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\biggl[x\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}-f_{2}(x)\biggr]
                      \end{equation*}
                      \begin{equation*}
                          \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} =  \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_{2}} -  \frac{5}{7Q_{2}}\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \biggl[x\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}-f_{2}(x)\biggr]_{x_{2}}
                      \end{equation*}
                      From eqn \ref{84},
                      \begin{equation*}
                            \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} =  \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_{2}} -  \frac{5}{7Q_{2}}\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta}
                      \end{equation*}
                      \begin{equation}
                          \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} =   \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} -   \frac{5N_{2}}{21\eta}\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_{2}}
                          \label{38}
                      \end{equation}
13.
                      \begin{equation*}
                          \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}=  \frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x}(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} -  \frac{5}{7\overline{Q_{2}}}\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}\biggl[x\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}-\overline{f_{2}(x)}\biggr]^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}
                      \end{equation*}
                      From eqns \ref{84} and \ref{85},
                      \begin{equation}
                       \begin{split} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} &=   \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{4}{7} \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_{2}} -  \frac{5}{7\overline{Q_{2}}}\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}\biggl[\frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta} + \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta} \\ & \quad - (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}\biggr]   +   \frac{5}{7\overline{Q_{2}}} \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)^2\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_{2}}\frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta}
                          \label{39}
                          \end{split}
                      \end{equation}
14.
                      \begin{equation*}
                          \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}}dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}=  \frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x}(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} -  \frac{5}{7Q_1}\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}}\biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}}
                      \end{equation*}
                      \begin{equation*}
                          = \frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x}(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_1} -  \frac{4}{7} \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -  \frac{5}{7Q_1}\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_1} \biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}-f_1(x)\biggr]_{x_1} +  \frac{5}{7Q_1}\frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}\biggl[x\frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x)\biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}} 
                      \end{equation*}
                      \begin{equation}                  \begin{split}   \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}}dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} &= \frac{4}{7}\sqrt{x}(f_1(x))^\frac{5}{2}\Bigg|_{x_1} -  \frac{4}{7}(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{5}{2} \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{5}{2} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}}- \frac{5}{7Q_1} \frac{df_1(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_1}\Biggl[\frac{N_1Q_1}{3\eta} + \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta} - (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{\overline{N_{2}}Q_1}{3\eta} \\ & \quad  +\biggl[(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} - (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}\biggr] \frac{N_{2}Q_1}{3\eta} - \gamma_{1\overline{2}}(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}\frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}   + \gamma_{1\overline{2}}\left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta}\Biggr] +  \frac{5}{7Q_1}(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^2
\frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \\ & \quad \biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_{2}}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}  + \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)\frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta} - (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \frac{N_{2}\overline{Q_{2}}}{3\eta}\biggr]
\end{split}
\label{40}
\end{equation}
15.
\begin{equation*}
    \frac{d^2f_{2}(x)}{dx^2} = Q_{2} \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    \frac{1}{Q_{2}}\frac{d^2f_{2}(x)}{dx^2} = \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \frac{1}{Q_{2}}\frac{d^2f_{2}(x)}{dx^2} = \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
    \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} =  \frac{1}{Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_{2}}
    \label{44}
\end{equation}
 16. 
                \begin{equation*}
                    \int^{x}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}
                \end{equation*}
                In region $0<x\leq x_{2}$, 
                \begin{equation}
                    = \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \Biggl[x \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} - f_{2}(x) \Biggr]
                    \label{78}
                \end{equation}
17. 
\begin{equation*}
   \int^{x}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
   = \int^{x}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{x}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{x}_{x_{2}} dx x \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]^{x}_{x_{2}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr] - \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} - f_{2}(x) \biggr]_{x_{2}} 
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
  = \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr] - \frac{N_{2}Q_{2}}{3\eta \overline{Q_{2}}}\left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)
    \label{56}
\end{equation}
 18. 
                \begin{equation}
                    \begin{split}
                        \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{x} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} & = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} \biggl( - \int^{x}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(f_2(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} \biggr)  \\ & = 0
                    \end{split}
                    \label{76}
                \end{equation}
                This is because $f_{2}(x)$ does not exist in region $> x_{2}$ \\
19. 
\begin{equation*}
    \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
In region $0<x\leq x_{2}$,
\begin{equation*}
    = \int^{x_{2}}_{x} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{x} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    = (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{x} dx \frac{1}{Q_{2}} \frac{d^2f_{2}(x)}{dx^2} + \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    =   \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\Bigg|^{x_{2}}_{x} +   \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
    =   \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{(\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} +   \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \frac{\left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx}\Bigg|_{x_{2}}
    \label{57}
\end{equation}
20.
\begin{equation*}
    \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
\end{equation*}
In region $x_{2}<x \leq \overline{x_{2}}$,
\begin{equation*}
    = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} dx \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}}\frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation*}
    =   \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x}
\end{equation*}
\begin{equation}
    =  \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}\Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{1}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}
    \label{58}
\end{equation}
    21. 
        \begin{equation*}
            \int^{x}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}
        \end{equation*}
        In region $0<x \leq x_{2}$, 
        \begin{equation*}
           = (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            = (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x}_0 dx \frac{x}{Q_{2}} \frac{d^2f_{2}(x)}{dx^2} 
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation*}
            = \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x}_0 dx x \frac{d^2f_{2}(x)}{dx^2} 
        \end{equation*}
        \begin{equation}
            = \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \biggl[ x\frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} - f_{2}(x)\biggr]
            \label{106}
        \end{equation}
        22. 
         In region $x_{2}<x\leq \overline{x_{2}}$,  
         \begin{equation*}
             = \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}
         \end{equation*}
         \begin{equation*}
             = (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} + (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} 
             \end{equation*}
             \begin{equation*}
                 = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{x}_{x_{2}} dx x \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2}
             \end{equation*}
             \begin{equation*}
                 = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]^{x}_{x_{2}} 
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                    = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr] - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]_{x_{2}}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation*}
                    = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr] - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right)}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} - f_{2}(x) \biggr]_{x_{2}}
                \end{equation*}
                \begin{equation}
                    = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]
                    \label{107}
                \end{equation}
                23. 
                 In region $\overline{x_{2}}<x\leq x_1$, 
      \begin{equation*}
          = \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
          = (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_0 dx \sqrt{x} (f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2} + (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \sqrt{x} (\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2} + \int^{x}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \sqrt{x} (f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2} 
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
          = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx x \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx x \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
          = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx}- \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]^{x}_{\overline{x_{2}}}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
          = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]_{x_{2}} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr] - \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
      \begin{split}
         &= \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \Biggl[ \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta} + \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta} \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) - \frac{N_{2} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \Biggr] + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr] \\ & \quad  - \frac{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} - \overline{f_{2}(x)} \biggr]_{\overline{x_{2}}}
         \end{split}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
      \begin{split}
          &= \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta \overline{Q_{2}}} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) - \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{1\overline{2}}  \\ & \quad - \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \gamma_{1\overline{2}}  + \frac{N_{2} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}}  + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]
          \end{split}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation}
          = \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} + \frac{\overline{N_{2}}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{\overline{N_{2}} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} - \frac{N_{2}}{3 \eta} (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} - \frac{N_{2} Q_{2}}{3 \eta Q_1} \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \gamma_{1\overline{2}} + \frac{N_{2} \overline{Q_{2}}}{3 \eta Q_1} (\beta_{\overline{2}2})^\frac{3}{2} \gamma_{1\overline{2}} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ x \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} - f_1(x) \biggr]
          \label{108}
      \end{equation}
      24. 
      \begin{equation*}
          \int^{x_1}_{x} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} 
      \end{equation*}
      In region $0<x \leq x_{2}$,
      \begin{equation*}
        = \int^{x_{2}}_{x} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}   
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
          = (\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{x_{2}}_{x} dx \frac{(f_{2}(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation*}
          = \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \int^{x_{2}}_{x} dx \frac{d^2f_{2}(x)}{dx^2} + \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x_{2}} dx \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2} 
      \end{equation*}
      \begin{equation}
          =   \frac{(\beta_{12})^\frac{3}{2}}{Q_{2}} \biggl[ \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} - \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \biggr] +   \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} -   \left(\frac{\beta_{12}}{\gamma_{1\overline{2}}}\right) \frac{df_{2}(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_{2}} \biggr] +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -  \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \biggr]
          \label{109}
      \end{equation}
      25. 
           In region $x_{2}<x\leq \overline{x_{2}}$,
           \begin{equation*}
               = \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation*}
               = (\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} dx \frac{(\overline{f_{2}(x)})^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}} + \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation*}
               = \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \int^{\overline{x_{2}}}_{x} dx \frac{d^2\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx^2} + \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{\overline{x_{2}}} dx \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2}
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation}
               =   \frac{(\gamma_{1\overline{2}})^\frac{3}{2}}{\overline{Q_{2}}} \biggl[ \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} - \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \biggr] +   \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} -  \gamma_{1\overline{2}} \frac{d\overline{f_{2}(x)}}{dx} \Bigg|_{\overline{x_{2}}} \biggr]
               \label{110}
           \end{equation}
           26. \\ 
           In region $\overline{x_{2}}<x\leq x_1$,
           \begin{equation*}
               \int^{x_1}_{x} dx \frac{(f_1(x))^\frac{3}{2}}{\sqrt{x}}
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation*}
               = \frac{1}{Q_1} \int^{x_1}_{x} dx \frac{d^2f_1(x)}{dx^2}
           \end{equation*}
           \begin{equation}
               =  \frac{1}{Q_1} \biggl[ \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \Bigg|_{x_1} - \frac{df_1(x)}{dx} \biggr]
               \label{111}
           \end{equation}
